(() => {
  const boot = window.__PATCHPAD_BOOT__ || { reports: [], activeReportId: null 
};
  const editorScroll = document.getElementById('editorScroll');
  const lineGutter = document.getElementById('lineGutter');
  const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
  const documentTitle = document.getElementById('documentTitle');
  const documentMeta = document.getElementById('documentMeta');
  const saveState = document.getElementById('saveState');
  const cursorState = document.getElementById('cursorState');
  const messageBar = document.getElementById('messageBar');
  const findBar = document.getElementById('findBar');
  const findInput = document.getElementById('findInput');
  const replaceInput = document.getElementById('replaceInput');
  const findCount = document.getElementById('findCount');
  const findStatus = document.getElementById('findStatus');
  const findToggleButton = document.getElementById('findToggleButton');
  const findPrevButton = document.getElementById('findPrevButton');
  const findNextButton = document.getElementById('findNextButton');
  const replaceCurrentButton = document.getElementById('replaceCurrentButton');
  const replaceAllButton = document.getElementById('replaceAllButton');
  const saveButton = document.getElementById('saveButton');
  const reloadButton = document.getElementById('reloadButton');
  const discardButton = document.getElementById('discardButton');
  const undoButton = document.getElementById('undoButton');
  const redoButton = document.getElementById('redoButton');
  const previewModal = document.getElementById('previewModal');
  const previewGutter = document.getElementById('previewGutter');
  const previewScroll = document.getElementById('previewScroll');
  const previewMeta = document.getElementById('previewMeta');
  const closePreviewButton = document.getElementById('closePreviewButton');
  const reportList = document.getElementById('report-list');

  const editorState = {
    reportId: boot.activeReportId,
    reportList: boot.reports,
    document: null,
    content: '',
    savedContent: '',
    baseRevision: 1,
    revisions: [],
    selectionRanges: [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }],
    undoStack: [],
    redoStack: [],
    lastEdit: null,
    lastRenderToken: 0,
    pendingScrollIntoView: false,
    findQuery: '',
    replaceText: '',
    matches: [],
    activeMatchIndex: -1,
    preview: null,
    editorWidth: 0,
    editorHeight: 0,
    rowHeight: 24,
    gutterWidth: 80,
    isLoading: false,
    status: 'Loading report…',
    message: '',
    conflict: null,
    lines: [''],
    lineStarts: [0],
  };

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  const measureContext = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const metricsCache = new Map();
  const segmenterGrapheme = typeof Intl !== 'undefined' && Intl.Segmenter ? new 
Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: 'grapheme' }) : null;
  const segmenterWord = typeof Intl !== 'undefined' && Intl.Segmenter ? new 
Intl.Segmenter(undefined, { granularity: 'word' }) : null;
  const TAB_SIZE = 4;
  const DOUBLE_CLICK_MS = 350;
  let scrollRaf = 0;
  let renderRaf = 0;
  let updateTimer = 0;
  let mouseDrag = null;
  let previewScrollState = { top: 0, left: 0 };

  function setStatus(text, kind = '') {
    editorState.status = text;
    saveState.textContent = text;
    saveState.className = kind ? `${kind}-text` : '';
  }

  function setMessage(text, kind = '') {
    editorState.message = text;
    messageBar.textContent = text;
    messageBar.className = kind ? `${kind}-text` : '';
  }

  function formatDate(iso) {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined, {
      dateStyle: 'medium',
      timeStyle: 'short',
    }).format(new Date(iso));
  }

  function cloneSelectionRanges(ranges) {
    return ranges.map((range) => ({ anchor: range.anchor, head: range.head }));
  }

  function normalizeRange(range) {
    return range.anchor <= range.head
      ? { start: range.anchor, end: range.head, anchor: range.anchor, head: 
range.head }
      : { start: range.head, end: range.anchor, anchor: range.anchor, head: 
range.head };
  }

  function sortRanges(ranges) {
    return ranges
      .map((range) => normalizeRange(range))
      .sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end);
  }

  function dedupeCollapsed(ranges) {
    const result = [];
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const range of ranges) {
      if (range.start === range.end) {
        const key = String(range.start);
        if (seen.has(key)) {
          continue;
        }
        seen.add(key);
      }
      result.push(range);
    }
    return result;
  }

  function setSelections(ranges, options = {}) {
    const nextRanges = dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(ranges));
    editorState.selectionRanges = nextRanges.length ? nextRanges : [{ anchor: 0,
head: 0 }];
    if (options.clearHistory !== false) {
      closeTypingGroup();
    }
    scheduleRender();
  }

  function primaryRange() {
    return normalizeRange(editorState.selectionRanges[0]);
  }

  function primaryHead() {
    return editorState.selectionRanges[0]?.head ?? 0;
  }

  function textLength() {
    return editorState.content.length;
  }

  function getLinesFromContent(content) {
    const lines = content.split('\n');
    if (lines.length === 0) {
      return [''];
    }
    return lines;
  }

  function buildLineStarts(lines) {
    const starts = [0];
    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
      const line = lines[index];
      const nextStart = starts[index] + line.length + (index < lines.length - 1 
? 1 : 0);
      starts.push(nextStart);
    }
    return starts;
  }

  function updateContentModel(content) {
    editorState.content = content;
    editorState.lines = getLinesFromContent(content);
    editorState.lineStarts = buildLineStarts(editorState.lines);
    metricsCache.clear();
  }

  function currentLineIndexForOffset(offset) {
    const starts = editorState.lineStarts;
    let low = 0;
    let high = starts.length - 2;
    while (low <= high) {
      const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
      if (starts[mid] <= offset && offset < starts[mid + 1]) {
        return mid;
      }
      if (offset < starts[mid]) {
        high = mid - 1;
      } else {
        low = mid + 1;
      }
    }
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(editorState.lines.length - 1, starts.length - 
2));
  }

  function lineInfoForOffset(offset) {
    const lineIndex = currentLineIndexForOffset(offset);
    const lineStart = editorState.lineStarts[lineIndex];
    const line = editorState.lines[lineIndex] ?? '';
    return {
      lineIndex,
      lineStart,
      line,
      columnOffset: offset - lineStart,
    };
  }

  function getGraphemeSegments(text) {
    if (segmenterGrapheme) {
      return Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(text), (segment) => 
segment.segment);
    }
    return Array.from(text);
  }

  function getWordSegments(text) {
    if (segmenterWord) {
      return Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(text));
    }
    const matches = [];
    const regex = /\b\w+\b/g;
    let match;
    while ((match = regex.exec(text))) {
      matches.push({ index: match.index, segment: match[0], isWordLike: true });
    }
    return matches;
  }

  function getLineMetrics(text) {
    const cached = metricsCache.get(text);
    if (cached) {
      return cached;
    }
    const font = getComputedStyle(editorScroll).font;
    measureContext.font = font;
    const graphemes = getGraphemeSegments(text);
    const offsets = [0];
    const widths = [0];
    let offset = 0;
    let width = 0;
    for (const grapheme of graphemes) {
      offset += grapheme.length;
      const display = grapheme === '\t' ? ' '.repeat(TAB_SIZE) : grapheme;
      width += measureContext.measureText(display).width;
      offsets.push(offset);
      widths.push(width);
    }
    const metrics = { offsets, widths, width };
    metricsCache.set(text, metrics);
    return metrics;
  }

  function clampOffset(offset) {
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(textLength(), offset));
  }

  function offsetToX(text, offset, metrics = null) {
    const actualMetrics = metrics || getLineMetrics(text);
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(offset, text.length));
    let low = 0;
    let high = actualMetrics.offsets.length - 1;
    while (low <= high) {
      const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
      const value = actualMetrics.offsets[mid];
      if (value === clamped) {
        return actualMetrics.widths[mid];
      }
      if (value < clamped) {
        low = mid + 1;
      } else {
        high = mid - 1;
      }
    }
    return actualMetrics.widths[Math.max(0, high)];
  }

  function xToOffset(text, x, metrics = null) {
    const actualMetrics = metrics || getLineMetrics(text);
    let low = 0;
    let high = actualMetrics.widths.length - 1;
    while (low <= high) {
      const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
      const value = actualMetrics.widths[mid];
      if (value === x) {
        return actualMetrics.offsets[mid];
      }
      if (value < x) {
        low = mid + 1;
      } else {
        high = mid - 1;
      }
    }
    const index = Math.max(0, Math.min(actualMetrics.offsets.length - 1, high));
    return actualMetrics.offsets[index];
  }

  function nextGraphemeOffset(text, offset, direction) {
    if (direction > 0) {
      if (offset >= text.length) {
        return text.length;
      }
      const remaining = text.slice(offset);
      const segments = getGraphemeSegments(remaining);
      return offset + (segments[0]?.length || 1);
    }
    if (offset <= 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    const head = text.slice(0, offset);
    const segments = getGraphemeSegments(head);
    return offset - (segments[segments.length - 1]?.length || 1);
  }

  function wordBoundary(text, offset, direction) {
    const wordSegments = getWordSegments(text);
    if (!wordSegments.length) {
      return direction > 0 ? text.length : 0;
    }
    if (direction > 0) {
      for (const segment of wordSegments) {
        const segmentEnd = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
        if (segment.index > offset) {
          return segment.index;
        }
        if (segment.index <= offset && offset < segmentEnd) {
          return segmentEnd;
        }
      }
      return text.length;
    }
    for (let index = wordSegments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      const segment = wordSegments[index];
      if (segment.index < offset && segment.isWordLike) {
        return segment.index;
      }
      if (segment.index <= offset && offset <= segment.index + 
segment.segment.length && segment.isWordLike) {
        return segment.index;
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }

  function offsetByLineColumn(lineIndex, column) {
    const line = editorState.lines[lineIndex] ?? '';
    const metrics = getLineMetrics(line);
    const safeColumn = Math.max(0, Math.min(column, line.length));
    const mappedOffset = metrics.offsets.find((value) => value === safeColumn);
    return editorState.lineStarts[lineIndex] + (mappedOffset ?? safeColumn);
  }

  function columnForOffset(offset) {
    const info = lineInfoForOffset(offset);
    return info.columnOffset;
  }

  function getCaretPosition(offset) {
    const info = lineInfoForOffset(offset);
    const lineMetrics = getLineMetrics(info.line);
    const x = offsetToX(info.line, info.columnOffset, lineMetrics);
    return {
      lineIndex: info.lineIndex,
      lineStart: info.lineStart,
      line,
      columnOffset: info.columnOffset,
      x,
      y: info.lineIndex * editorState.rowHeight,
    };
  }

  function replaceSelections(ranges, replacementFactory, options = {}) {
    const sorted = sortRanges(ranges);
    if (!sorted.length) {
      return;
    }

    const before = snapshotEditorState();
    let content = editorState.content;
    const nextSelections = [];
    for (let index = sorted.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      const range = sorted[index];
      const replacement = typeof replacementFactory === 'function' ? 
replacementFactory(range, index) : replacementFactory;
      content = content.slice(0, range.start) + replacement + 
content.slice(range.end);
      nextSelections.unshift({ anchor: range.start + replacement.length, head: 
range.start + replacement.length });
    }

    updateContentModel(content);
    editorState.selectionRanges = nextSelections;
    afterEdit(before, { kind: options.kind || 'edit', merge: options.merge !== 
false });
    selectActiveMatchForRange(nextSelections[0] || { anchor: 0, head: 0 });
    scheduleRender();
    scheduleSaveStatus();
  }

  function snapshotEditorState() {
    return {
      content: editorState.content,
      selectionRanges: cloneSelectionRanges(editorState.selectionRanges),
      findQuery: editorState.findQuery,
      replaceText: editorState.replaceText,
      activeMatchIndex: editorState.activeMatchIndex,
    };
  }

  function restoreSnapshot(snapshot) {
    updateContentModel(snapshot.content);
    editorState.selectionRanges = 
cloneSelectionRanges(snapshot.selectionRanges);
    editorState.findQuery = snapshot.findQuery;
    editorState.replaceText = snapshot.replaceText;
    editorState.activeMatchIndex = snapshot.activeMatchIndex;
    recomputeFindMatches();
    renderFindBar();
    renderReports();
    renderEditor();
    renderHistory();
    renderStatus();
  }

  function openUndoGroup(snapshot) {
    if (editorState.lastEdit && Date.now() - editorState.lastEdit.time < 850 && 
editorState.lastEdit.merge) {
      return;
    }
    editorState.undoStack.push(snapshot);
    editorState.redoStack.length = 0;
  }

  function closeTypingGroup() {
    editorState.lastEdit = null;
  }

  function afterEdit(snapshot, options = {}) {
    const merge = options.merge !== false;
    const kind = options.kind || 'edit';
    const now = Date.now();
    const last = editorState.lastEdit;
    const canMerge = merge && last && last.kind === kind && now - last.time < 
850;
    if (!canMerge) {
      editorState.undoStack.push(snapshot);
      editorState.redoStack.length = 0;
    }
    editorState.lastEdit = {
      kind,
      time: now,
      merge,
      selectionHead: primaryHead(),
    };
  }

  function undo() {
    if (!editorState.undoStack.length) {
      setMessage('Nothing to undo.');
      return;
    }
    const current = snapshotEditorState();
    const snapshot = editorState.undoStack.pop();
    editorState.redoStack.push(current);
    restoreSnapshot(snapshot);
    setDirtyIndicators();
    setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
  }

  function redo() {
    if (!editorState.redoStack.length) {
      setMessage('Nothing to redo.');
      return;
    }
    const current = snapshotEditorState();
    const snapshot = editorState.redoStack.pop();
    editorState.undoStack.push(current);
    restoreSnapshot(snapshot);
    setDirtyIndicators();
    setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
  }

  function setDirtyIndicators() {
    const isDirty = editorState.content !== editorState.savedContent;
    if (editorState.conflict) {
      setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${editorState.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
      return;
    }
    if (isDirty) {
      setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${editorState.baseRevision}`, 
'warning');
    } else {
      setStatus(`Saved at revision ${editorState.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
    }
  }

  function currentSelectionText(range = primaryRange()) {
    return editorState.content.slice(range.start, range.end);
  }

  function selectActiveMatchForRange(range) {
    if (!editorState.findQuery) {
      return;
    }
    const index = editorState.matches.findIndex((match) => match.start === 
range.start && match.end === range.end);
    if (index >= 0) {
      editorState.activeMatchIndex = index;
    }
  }

  function recomputeFindMatches() {
    const query = editorState.findQuery;
    if (!query) {
      editorState.matches = [];
      editorState.activeMatchIndex = -1;
      renderFindBar();
      return;
    }
    const matches = [];
    let index = editorState.content.indexOf(query);
    while (index !== -1) {
      matches.push({ start: index, end: index + query.length });
      index = editorState.content.indexOf(query, index + Math.max(1, 
query.length));
    }
    editorState.matches = matches;
    if (!matches.length) {
      editorState.activeMatchIndex = -1;
    } else if (editorState.activeMatchIndex < 0 || editorState.activeMatchIndex 
>= matches.length) {
      editorState.activeMatchIndex = 0;
    }
    renderFindBar();
    if (matches.length) {
      const active = matches[editorState.activeMatchIndex];
      editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: active.start, head: active.end 
}];
      scheduleRender();
      scrollSelectionIntoView();
    }
  }

  function navigateMatches(direction) {
    if (!editorState.matches.length) {
      setMessage('No matches found.', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    const total = editorState.matches.length;
    const current = editorState.activeMatchIndex < 0 ? 0 : 
editorState.activeMatchIndex;
    const next = (current + direction + total) % total;
    editorState.activeMatchIndex = next;
    const match = editorState.matches[next];
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }];
    scheduleRender();
    scrollSelectionIntoView();
    renderFindBar();
    setMessage(`Match ${next + 1} of ${total}.`, 'good');
  }

  function replaceCurrentMatch() {
    if (!editorState.matches.length) {
      setMessage('No match to replace.', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    const active = editorState.matches[editorState.activeMatchIndex >= 0 ? 
editorState.activeMatchIndex : 0];
    if (!active) {
      setMessage('No match to replace.', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    const replacement = replaceInput.value;
    const before = snapshotEditorState();
    updateContentModel(editorState.content.slice(0, active.start) + replacement 
+ editorState.content.slice(active.end));
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: active.start + replacement.length, 
head: active.start + replacement.length }];
    afterEdit(before, { kind: 'replace-current', merge: false });
    recomputeFindMatches();
    setDirtyIndicators();
    setMessage('Replaced the current match.', 'good');
  }

  function replaceAllMatches() {
    if (!editorState.matches.length) {
      setMessage('No matches to replace.', 'warning');
      return;
    }
    const before = snapshotEditorState();
    const replacement = replaceInput.value;
    let content = editorState.content;
    for (let index = editorState.matches.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
      const match = editorState.matches[index];
      content = content.slice(0, match.start) + replacement + 
content.slice(match.end);
    }
    updateContentModel(content);
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
    afterEdit(before, { kind: 'replace-all', merge: false });
    recomputeFindMatches();
    setDirtyIndicators();
    setMessage(`Replaced all ${before.matchCount || editorState.matches.length} 
matches.`, 'good');
  }

  function lineSelectionForRange(range) {
    const startInfo = lineInfoForOffset(range.start);
    const endInfo = lineInfoForOffset(range.end);
    return { startInfo, endInfo };
  }

  function blockRangeFromSelection(range) {
    const startInfo = lineInfoForOffset(range.start);
    const endInfo = lineInfoForOffset(range.end);
    const start = startInfo.lineStart;
    let end = endInfo.lineStart + endInfo.line.length;
    if (range.end > endInfo.lineStart + endInfo.line.length && endInfo.lineIndex
< editorState.lines.length - 1) {
      end = editorState.lineStarts[endInfo.lineIndex + 1];
    }
    return { start, end, startLine: startInfo.lineIndex, endLine: 
endInfo.lineIndex };
  }

  function indentSelection(outdent = false) {
    const range = primaryRange();
    const block = blockRangeFromSelection(range);
    const lines = editorState.lines.slice(block.startLine, block.endLine + 1);
    if (!lines.length) {
      return;
    }
    const before = snapshotEditorState();
    let added = 0;
    const selectedLineCount = lines.length;
    const adjusted = lines.map((line) => {
      if (outdent) {
        if (line.startsWith('\t')) {
          added -= 1;
          return line.slice(1);
        }
        if (line.startsWith(' '.repeat(TAB_SIZE))) {
          added -= TAB_SIZE;
          return line.slice(TAB_SIZE);
        }
        if (line.startsWith(' ')) {
          const match = line.match(/^ +/)[0].length;
          const remove = Math.min(TAB_SIZE, match);
          added -= remove;
          return line.slice(remove);
        }
        return line;
      }
      added += TAB_SIZE;
      return `\t${line}`;
    });
    const startOffset = editorState.lineStarts[block.startLine];
    const endOffset = block.endLine < editorState.lines.length - 1 ? 
editorState.lineStarts[block.endLine + 1] : editorState.content.length;
    updateContentModel(editorState.content.slice(0, startOffset) + 
adjusted.join('\n') + editorState.content.slice(endOffset));
    const newStart = startOffset;
    const newEnd = startOffset + adjusted.join('\n').length + (block.endLine < 
editorState.lines.length - 1 ? 1 : 0);
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: newStart, head: newEnd }];
    afterEdit(before, { kind: outdent ? 'outdent' : 'indent', merge: false });
    setDirtyIndicators();
    setMessage(outdent ? 'Outdented the selected block.' : 'Indented the 
selected block.', 'good');
  }

  function deleteSelectionOrGrapheme(direction) {
    const ranges = cloneSelectionRanges(editorState.selectionRanges);
    const selectedRanges = sortRanges(ranges).map((range) => {
      if (range.start !== range.end) {
        return range;
      }
      const offset = range.start;
      if (direction < 0) {
        return { start: nextGraphemeOffset(editorState.content, offset, -1), 
end: offset, anchor: offset, head: offset };
      }
      return { start: offset, end: nextGraphemeOffset(editorState.content, 
offset, 1), anchor: offset, head: offset };
    });
    replaceSelections(selectedRanges, '', { kind: direction < 0 ? 'backspace' : 
'delete' });
  }

  function moveSelection(direction, options = {}) {
    const byWord = options.byWord || false;
    const allRanges = editorState.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
      const normalized = normalizeRange(range);
      const movingFrom = options.shift
        ? range.head
        : direction > 0
          ? normalized.end
          : normalized.start;
      const nextOffset = byWord
        ? wordBoundary(editorState.content, movingFrom, direction)
        : nextGraphemeOffset(editorState.content, movingFrom, direction);
      if (options.shift) {
        return { anchor: range.anchor, head: nextOffset };
      }
      return { anchor: nextOffset, head: nextOffset };
    });
    editorState.selectionRanges = allRanges;
    editorState.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(editorState.selectionRanges));
    if (!options.preserveFind) {
      editorState.activeMatchIndex = findCurrentMatchIndexFromSelection();
    }
    closeTypingGroup();
    scheduleRender();
    scrollSelectionIntoView();
  }

  function moveVertical(direction, shift) {
    const nextRanges = editorState.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
      const normalized = normalizeRange(range);
      const anchor = range.anchor;
      const caret = shift ? range.head : normalized.end;
      const info = lineInfoForOffset(caret);
      const targetLine = Math.max(0, Math.min(editorState.lines.length - 1, 
info.lineIndex + direction));
      const targetColumn = columnForOffset(caret);
      const targetLineText = editorState.lines[targetLine] || '';
      const targetMetrics = getLineMetrics(targetLineText);
      const targetOffsetWithinLine = xToOffset(targetLineText, 
offsetToX(info.line, targetColumn, getLineMetrics(info.line)), targetMetrics);
      const nextOffset = editorState.lineStarts[targetLine] + 
targetOffsetWithinLine;
      return shift ? { anchor, head: nextOffset } : { anchor: nextOffset, head: 
nextOffset };
    });
    editorState.selectionRanges = dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(nextRanges));
    closeTypingGroup();
    scheduleRender();
    scrollSelectionIntoView();
  }

  function findCurrentMatchIndexFromSelection() {
    const current = primaryRange();
    return editorState.matches.findIndex((match) => match.start === 
current.start && match.end === current.end);
  }

  function selectAll() {
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: editorState.content.length
}];
    closeTypingGroup();
    scheduleRender();
    scrollSelectionIntoView();
    setMessage('Selected the entire report.');
  }

  function insertText(text) {
    const ranges = sortRanges(editorState.selectionRanges).map((range) => {
      if (range.start === range.end) {
        return range;
      }
      return range;
    });
    replaceSelections(ranges, text, { kind: 'insert', merge: true });
  }

  function handleTabKey(shiftKey) {
    const range = primaryRange();
    const multiline = range.start !== range.end && 
currentSelectionText(range).includes('\n');
    if (multiline) {
      indentSelection(shiftKey);
      return true;
    }
    if (shiftKey) {
      indentSelection(true);
      return true;
    }
    insertText('\t');
    return true;
  }

  function saveReport() {
    if (!editorState.document) {
      return;
    }
    const body = {
      documentId: editorState.document.id,
      baseRevision: editorState.baseRevision,
      content: editorState.content,
    };
    setMessage('Saving report…');
    fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(editorState.document.id)}/save`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
        if (response.status === 409) {
          editorState.conflict = data.document || { currentRevision: 'unknown' 
};
          setDirtyIndicators();
          setMessage('Save conflict: another tab saved a newer version. Your 
draft is still here.', 'warning');
          return null;
        }
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed.');
        }
        return data;
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (!data) {
          return;
        }
        editorState.conflict = null;
        if (data.document) {
          editorState.savedContent = data.document.content;
          editorState.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
          editorState.document.currentRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
          editorState.document.updatedAt = data.document.updatedAt;
        }
        setDirtyIndicators();
        renderHistory();
        renderReports();
        setMessage(data.changed ? `Saved as revision 
${editorState.baseRevision}.` : 'No changes to save.', 'good');
      })
      .catch((error) => {
        setMessage(error.message, 'error');
      });
  }

  function reloadLatest({ discard = false } = {}) {
    if (!editorState.document) {
      return;
    }
    loadReport(editorState.document.id, { preserveFind: false, discardDraft: 
discard });
  }

  function renderReports() {
    if (!reportList) {
      return;
    }
    const buttons = Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip'));
    for (const button of buttons) {
      button.classList.toggle('active', button.dataset.reportId === 
editorState.reportId);
      button.addEventListener('click', () => {
        loadReport(button.dataset.reportId);
      }, { once: true });
    }
  }

  function renderHeader() {
    if (!editorState.document) {
      documentTitle.textContent = 'Loading…';
      documentMeta.textContent = '';
      return;
    }
    documentTitle.textContent = editorState.document.title;
    documentMeta.textContent = `Author: ${editorState.document.author} • 
${editorState.document.summary}`;
  }

  function renderHistory() {
    if (!editorState.revisions.length) {
      historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
      return;
    }
    historyList.innerHTML = editorState.revisions
      .map((revision) => `
        <article class="history-entry">
          <div>
            <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
            <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
          </div>
          <div class="history-entry-actions">
            <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
            <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
          </div>
        </article>
      `)
      .join('');
    for (const button of 
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]')) {
      button.addEventListener('click', () => 
previewRevision(button.dataset.previewRevision), { once: true });
    }
    for (const button of 
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]')) {
      button.addEventListener('click', () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision), { once: true });
    }
  }

  function renderFindBar() {
    const total = editorState.matches.length;
    findCount.textContent = `${total} match${total === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
    if (!editorState.findQuery) {
      findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
      return;
    }
    if (!total) {
      findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
      return;
    }
    findStatus.textContent = `Match ${editorState.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${total}`;
  }

  function renderStatus() {
    setDirtyIndicators();
    const range = primaryRange();
    const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
    const columnText = `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column 
${visibleColumnForOffset(info.line, info.columnOffset) + 1}`;
    cursorState.textContent = editorState.selectionRanges.length > 1
      ? `${columnText} • ${editorState.selectionRanges.length} carets`
      : columnText;
  }

  function visibleColumnForOffset(lineText, offset) {
    return getGraphemeSegments(lineText.slice(0, offset)).length;
  }

  function ensureEditorDimensions() {
    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
    editorState.editorWidth = rect.width;
    editorState.editorHeight = rect.height;
    editorState.gutterWidth = lineGutter.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  }

  function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, content, 
options = {}) {
    const totalLines = content.lines.length;
    const rowHeight = editorState.rowHeight;
    const viewportHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
    const scrollTop = options.scrollTop ?? scrollContainer.scrollTop;
    const scrollLeft = options.scrollLeft ?? scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 2);
    const endLine = Math.min(totalLines - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop + 
viewportHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
    const spacerHeight = totalLines * rowHeight;

    const renderedRows = [];
    const gutterRows = [];
    const selectionRanges = content.selectionRanges || [];
    const findMatches = content.matches || [];
    const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;

    for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 1) {
      const lineText = content.lines[lineIndex] ?? '';
      const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] ?? 0;
      const metrics = getLineMetrics(lineText);
      const lineTop = lineIndex * rowHeight;
      const selectRects = [];
      const matchRects = [];
      const caretRects = [];

      for (const range of selectionRanges) {
        const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
        const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + lineText.length);
        if (start < end) {
          const x1 = offsetToX(lineText, start - lineStart, metrics);
          const x2 = offsetToX(lineText, end - lineStart, metrics);
          selectRects.push({ left: x1, width: Math.max(1, x2 - x1) });
        }
        if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && range.start
<= lineStart + lineText.length) {
          const x = offsetToX(lineText, range.start - lineStart, metrics);
          caretRects.push({ left: x });
        }
      }

      for (let matchIndex = 0; matchIndex < findMatches.length; matchIndex += 1)
{
        const match = findMatches[matchIndex];
        const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
        const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + lineText.length);
        if (start < end) {
          const x1 = offsetToX(lineText, start - lineStart, metrics);
          const x2 = offsetToX(lineText, end - lineStart, metrics);
          matchRects.push({ left: x1, width: Math.max(1, x2 - x1), current: 
matchIndex === activeMatchIndex });
        }
      }

      renderedRows.push(`
        <div class="line-row" style="top:${lineTop}px; height:${rowHeight}px;">
          ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div class="match-rect${rect.current ? ' 
current-match' : ''}" style="left:${rect.left}px; 
width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
          ${selectRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
          ${caretRects.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
          <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(lineText)}</span>
        </div>
      `);
      gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${lineTop}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
    }

    scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${spacerHeight}px; min-width:${Math.max(100, content.maxLineWidth 
|| 0) + 140}px;">${renderedRows.join('')}</div>`;
    gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${spacerHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
    scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
    scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
  }

  function renderPreview(revision) {
    const previewContent = {
      lines: getLinesFromContent(revision.content),
      lineStarts: buildLineStarts(getLinesFromContent(revision.content)),
      selectionRanges: [],
      matches: [],
      activeMatchIndex: -1,
      maxLineWidth: 0,
    };
    const lineWidths = previewContent.lines.map((line) => 
getLineMetrics(line).width);
    previewContent.maxLineWidth = Math.max(...lineWidths, 0);
    renderEditorSurface(previewScroll, previewGutter, previewContent, 
previewScrollState);
  }

  function renderEditor() {
    if (!editorState.document) {
      return;
    }
    ensureEditorDimensions();
    const lineWidths = editorState.lines.map((line) => 
getLineMetrics(line).width);
    const maxLineWidth = Math.max(...lineWidths, 0);
    renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
      lines: editorState.lines,
      lineStarts: editorState.lineStarts,
      selectionRanges: editorState.selectionRanges,
      matches: editorState.matches,
      activeMatchIndex: editorState.activeMatchIndex,
      maxLineWidth,
    });
    renderStatus();
  }

  function scheduleRender() {
    if (renderRaf) {
      return;
    }
    renderRaf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      renderRaf = 0;
      renderEditor();
      if (editorState.preview) {
        renderPreview(editorState.preview);
      }
    });
  }

  function scheduleScrollRender() {
    if (scrollRaf) {
      return;
    }
    scrollRaf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      scrollRaf = 0;
      if (editorState.preview) {
        renderPreview(editorState.preview);
      } else {
        renderEditor();
      }
    });
  }

  function scheduleSaveStatus() {
    clearTimeout(updateTimer);
    updateTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      setDirtyIndicators();
    }, 50);
  }

  function scrollSelectionIntoView() {
    const range = primaryRange();
    const info = getCaretPosition(range.head);
    const top = info.y;
    const bottom = top + editorState.rowHeight;
    const visibleTop = editorScroll.scrollTop;
    const visibleBottom = visibleTop + editorScroll.clientHeight;
    if (top < visibleTop + 8) {
      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, top - 20 * editorState.rowHeight);
    } else if (bottom > visibleBottom - 8) {
      editorScroll.scrollTop = bottom - editorScroll.clientHeight + 24;
    }
    const lineWidth = getLineMetrics(info.line).width;
    const caretX = info.x + 16;
    const visibleLeft = editorScroll.scrollLeft;
    const visibleRight = visibleLeft + editorScroll.clientWidth;
    if (caretX < visibleLeft + 24) {
      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 120);
    } else if (caretX > visibleRight - 24) {
      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - editorScroll.clientWidth + 
160);
    }
  }

  function getOffsetFromClientPoint(clientX, clientY) {
    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + clientX - rect.left - 0;
    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + clientY - rect.top;
    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(editorState.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / editorState.rowHeight)));
    const lineText = editorState.lines[lineIndex] || '';
    const metrics = getLineMetrics(lineText);
    const lineX = x - 0;
    const offsetWithinLine = xToOffset(lineText, Math.max(0, lineX), metrics);
    return editorState.lineStarts[lineIndex] + offsetWithinLine;
  }

  function selectWordAtOffset(offset) {
    const info = lineInfoForOffset(offset);
    const line = info.line;
    const lineOffset = offset - info.lineStart;
    const segments = segmenterWord ? Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line)) : 
[];
    if (!segments.length) {
      return { anchor: info.lineStart, head: info.lineStart + line.length };
    }
    let selected = null;
    for (const segment of segments) {
      const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
      if (segment.index <= lineOffset && lineOffset <= end && 
segment.isWordLike) {
        selected = { start: segment.index, end };
        break;
      }
      if (lineOffset < segment.index) {
        selected = { start: segment.index, end: segment.index + 
segment.segment.length };
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!selected) {
      for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
        const segment = segments[index];
        if (segment.isWordLike) {
          selected = { start: segment.index, end: segment.index + 
segment.segment.length };
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!selected) {
      return { anchor: info.lineStart, head: info.lineStart + line.length };
    }
    return { anchor: info.lineStart + selected.start, head: info.lineStart + 
selected.end };
  }

  function selectLineAtOffset(offset) {
    const info = lineInfoForOffset(offset);
    return { anchor: info.lineStart, head: info.lineStart + info.line.length };
  }

  function beginMouseSelection(event) {
    const offset = getOffsetFromClientPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY);
    if (event.altKey || event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) {
      editorState.selectionRanges = [...editorState.selectionRanges, { anchor: 
offset, head: offset }];
      closeTypingGroup();
      scheduleRender();
      return;
    }

    let nextSelection = { anchor: offset, head: offset };
    if (event.detail >= 3) {
      nextSelection = selectLineAtOffset(offset);
    } else if (event.detail === 2) {
      nextSelection = selectWordAtOffset(offset);
    }
    editorState.selectionRanges = [nextSelection];
    mouseDrag = { start: offset, active: true };
    closeTypingGroup();
    scheduleRender();
  }

  function updateMouseSelection(event) {
    if (!mouseDrag || !mouseDrag.active) {
      return;
    }
    const offset = getOffsetFromClientPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY);
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: mouseDrag.start, head: offset }];
    scheduleRender();
    scrollSelectionIntoView();
  }

  function endMouseSelection() {
    if (mouseDrag) {
      mouseDrag.active = false;
      mouseDrag = null;
    }
  }

  async function loadReport(reportId, options = {}) {
    if (!reportId) {
      return;
    }
    editorState.isLoading = true;
    setStatus('Loading report…');
    setMessage('');
    const response = await 
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`);
    if (!response.ok) {
      setMessage(`Failed to load report ${reportId}.`, 'error');
      editorState.isLoading = false;
      return;
    }
    const data = await response.json();
    editorState.reportId = data.document.id;
    editorState.document = data.document;
    editorState.reportList = boot.reports;
    editorState.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
    editorState.savedContent = data.document.content;
    editorState.revisions = data.revisions;
    editorState.conflict = null;
    editorState.undoStack = [];
    editorState.redoStack = [];
    editorState.findQuery = options.preserveFind ? editorState.findQuery : '';
    editorState.replaceText = editorState.replaceText || '';
    if (!options.discardDraft) {
      updateContentModel(data.document.content);
    } else {
      updateContentModel(data.document.content);
    }
    editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
    editorState.matches = [];
    editorState.activeMatchIndex = -1;
    editorState.lastEdit = null;
    editorState.isLoading = false;
    renderHeader();
    renderHistory();
    renderReports();
    renderFindBar();
    setDirtyIndicators();
    renderEditor();
    setMessage(options.discardDraft ? 'Reloaded the latest saved report.' : 
'Report loaded.', 'good');
    const activeButton = 
Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip')).find((button) => 
button.dataset.reportId === reportId);
    if (activeButton) {
      activeButton.classList.add('active');
    }
  }

  function previewRevision(revisionNumber) {
    fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(editorState.document.id)}/revisions
/${encodeURIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data })))
      .then(({ response, data }) => {
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to load revision preview.');
        }
        editorState.preview = {
          revisionNumber: data.revisionNumber,
          content: data.content,
          createdAt: data.createdAt,
        };
        previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
        previewModal.hidden = false;
        previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
        previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
        renderPreview(editorState.preview);
      })
      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
  }

  function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
    fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(editorState.document.id)}/revisions
/${encodeURIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data })))
      .then(({ response, data }) => {
        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
        }
        const before = snapshotEditorState();
        updateContentModel(data.content);
        editorState.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
        afterEdit(before, { kind: 'restore-revision', merge: false });
        recomputeFindMatches();
        setDirtyIndicators();
        renderEditor();
        setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the editor. 
Save to create a new revision.`, 'warning');
      })
      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
  }

  function closePreview() {
    editorState.preview = null;
    previewModal.hidden = true;
    editorScroll.focus();
  }

  function renderFindSelectionIfNeeded() {
    if (!editorState.findQuery) {
      editorState.matches = [];
      editorState.activeMatchIndex = -1;
      return;
    }
    recomputeFindMatches();
  }

  function handleFindInputChange() {
    editorState.findQuery = findInput.value;
    recomputeFindMatches();
    if (editorState.matches.length) {
      navigateMatches(0);
    } else {
      setMessage('No matches found.', 'warning');
    }
  }

  function keyboardShortcut(event) {
    const isMac = navigator.platform.toLowerCase().includes('mac');
    const mod = isMac ? event.metaKey : event.ctrlKey;
    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();

    if (mod && key === 'f') {
      event.preventDefault();
      findBar.hidden = false;
      findInput.focus();
      findInput.select();
      return true;
    }
    if (mod && key === 's') {
      event.preventDefault();
      saveReport();
      return true;
    }
    if (mod && key === 'z' && !event.shiftKey) {
      event.preventDefault();
      undo();
      return true;
    }
    if (mod && (key === 'y' || (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey))) {
      event.preventDefault();
      redo();
      return true;
    }
    if (mod && key === 'a') {
      event.preventDefault();
      selectAll();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function handleEditorKeydown(event) {
    if (keyboardShortcut(event)) {
      return;
    }

    const mod = event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey;
    const shift = event.shiftKey;
    const key = event.key;

    if (key === 'Escape') {
      event.preventDefault();
      findInput.focus();
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'Tab') {
      event.preventDefault();
      handleTabKey(shift);
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'Enter') {
      event.preventDefault();
      insertText('\n');
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'Backspace') {
      event.preventDefault();
      deleteSelectionOrGrapheme(-1);
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'Delete') {
      event.preventDefault();
      deleteSelectionOrGrapheme(1);
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') {
      event.preventDefault();
      moveSelection(-1, { shift, byWord: mod });
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'ArrowRight') {
      event.preventDefault();
      moveSelection(1, { shift, byWord: mod });
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'ArrowUp') {
      event.preventDefault();
      moveVertical(-1, shift);
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'ArrowDown') {
      event.preventDefault();
      moveVertical(1, shift);
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'Home') {
      event.preventDefault();
      const ranges = editorState.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
        const next = mod ? 0 : info.lineStart;
        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: next } : { anchor: next, 
head: next };
      });
      editorState.selectionRanges = dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(ranges));
      scheduleRender();
      scrollSelectionIntoView();
      return;
    }
    if (key === 'End') {
      event.preventDefault();
      const ranges = editorState.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
        const next = mod ? editorState.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: next } : { anchor: next, 
head: next };
      });
      editorState.selectionRanges = dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(ranges));
      scheduleRender();
      scrollSelectionIntoView();
      return;
    }
    if (mod && key === 'g') {
      event.preventDefault();
      navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
      return;
    }
    if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
      event.preventDefault();
      insertText(key);
    }
  }

  function handlePaste(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const text = event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') ?? '';
    if (text) {
      insertText(text);
    }
  }

  function handleCopy(event) {
    const range = primaryRange();
    if (range.start === range.end) {
      return;
    }
    event.preventDefault();
    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', currentSelectionText(range));
  }

  function handleCut(event) {
    const range = primaryRange();
    if (range.start === range.end) {
      return;
    }
    event.preventDefault();
    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', currentSelectionText(range));
    replaceSelections([range], '', { kind: 'cut', merge: false });
  }

  function handleFindKeyboard(event) {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
      event.preventDefault();
      editorScroll.focus();
      return;
    }
    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
      event.preventDefault();
      navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
      return;
    }
  }

  function handleEditorFocus() {
    renderStatus();
  }

  function handleScroll() {
    if (editorState.preview) {
      previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
      scheduleScrollRender();
      return;
    }
    scheduleScrollRender();
  }

  function handlePreviewScroll() {
    previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
    scheduleScrollRender();
  }

  function init() {
    editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeydown);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('focus', handleEditorFocus);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', beginMouseSelection);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', updateMouseSelection);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('mouseup', endMouseSelection);
    editorScroll.addEventListener('mouseleave', endMouseSelection);
    findInput.addEventListener('input', handleFindInputChange);
    findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindKeyboard);
    replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
        editorScroll.focus();
      }
    });
    findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
      findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
      if (!findBar.hidden) {
        findInput.focus();
        findInput.select();
      } else {
        editorScroll.focus();
      }
    });
    findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
    findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
    replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
    replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
    saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
    reloadButton.addEventListener('click', () => reloadLatest({ discard: true 
}));
    discardButton.addEventListener('click', () => reloadLatest({ discard: true 
}));
    undoButton.addEventListener('click', undo);
    redoButton.addEventListener('click', redo);
    closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
    previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
      if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
        closePreview();
      }
    });
    previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);

    renderReports();
    loadReport(editorState.reportId || boot.activeReportId).then(() => {
      editorScroll.focus();
      scheduleRender();
    });
  }

  // Additional editor interactions.
  editorScroll.addEventListener('dragstart', (event) => event.preventDefault());

  // Keep rendering stable when the window size changes.
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
    metricsCache.clear();
    scheduleRender();
  });

  function escapeHtml(value) {
    return String(value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
  }

  init();
})();
EOF

# PatchPad App Manifest

- Start command: `npm start`
- SQLite path: `/app/patchpad.sqlite`

## Routes and methods

- `GET /` — open the PatchPad editor shell and report list
- `GET /api/reports` — browse available reports
- `GET /api/reports/:documentId` — open a report with its current saved content 
and revision list
- `GET /api/reports/:documentId/revisions` — browse revision history for one 
report
- `GET /api/reports/:documentId/revisions/:revisionNumber` — preview a saved 
revision
- `POST /api/reports/:documentId/save` — save the open report with 
`baseRevision` and text content
EOF
(6) cat > /app/AGENTS.md <<'EOF'
# Repository Notes

- PatchPad uses `/app/patchpad.sqlite` as the local source of truth.
- The seeded report is `incident-alpha` and starts at revision 1.
- Save requests must include `baseRevision` and text `content`; stale saves 
return HTTP 409.
- The browser editor is custom DOM-based and does not use 
textarea/contenteditable for the document surface.
EOF

cat > /app/server.js <<'EOF'
const express = require('express');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { DatabaseSync } = require('node:sqlite');

const APP_ROOT = __dirname;
const DB_PATH = path.join(APP_ROOT, 'patchpad.sqlite');
const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3000);
const SEED_PATH = '/assets/incident_seed.json';

const app = express();
const db = new DatabaseSync(DB_PATH);

app.use(express.json({ limit: '2mb' }));
app.use(express.static(path.join(APP_ROOT, 'public')));

function isoNow() {
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

function loadSeed() {
  return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SEED_PATH, 'utf8'));
}

function documentTextFromSeed(seed) {
  const doc = seed.document;
  const generatedLines = Array.from({ length: doc.generatedLineCount }, (_, 
index) =>
    doc.generatedLineTemplate.replace('{n}', String(index + 1))
  );
  return [...doc.sections, ...generatedLines, ...doc.tailSections].join('\n');
}

function initializeSchema() {
  db.exec(`
    PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
    PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS documents (
      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
      title TEXT NOT NULL,
      author TEXT NOT NULL,
      summary TEXT NOT NULL,
      content TEXT NOT NULL,
      current_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
    );
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revisions (
      id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
      document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
      revision_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
      content TEXT NOT NULL,
      created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
      UNIQUE(document_id, revision_number),
      FOREIGN KEY(document_id) REFERENCES documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
    );
  `);
}

function seedIfNeeded() {
  const count = db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM 
documents').get().count;
  if (count > 0) {
    return;
  }

  const seed = loadSeed();
  const doc = seed.document;
  const content = documentTextFromSeed(seed);
  const now = isoNow();

  db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
  try {
    db.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO documents (id, title, author, summary, content, 
current_revision, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)'
    ).run(doc.id, doc.title, doc.author, doc.summary, content, 1, now, now);
    db.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO revisions (document_id, revision_number, content, created_at)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)'
    ).run(doc.id, 1, content, now);
    db.exec('COMMIT');
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      db.exec('ROLLBACK');
    } catch {
      // ignore rollback errors
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

function listDocuments() {
  return db.prepare(
    'SELECT id, title, author, summary, current_revision AS currentRevision, 
updated_at AS updatedAt FROM documents ORDER BY title ASC'
  ).all();
}

function getDocument(documentId) {
  return db.prepare(
    'SELECT id, title, author, summary, content, current_revision AS 
currentRevision, created_at AS createdAt, updated_at AS updatedAt FROM documents
WHERE id = ?'
  ).get(documentId);
}

function getRevisionHistory(documentId) {
  return db.prepare(
    'SELECT revision_number AS revisionNumber, created_at AS createdAt FROM 
revisions WHERE document_id = ? ORDER BY revision_number DESC'
  ).all(documentId);
}

function getRevision(documentId, revisionNumber) {
  return db.prepare(
    'SELECT document_id AS documentId, revision_number AS revisionNumber, 
content, created_at AS createdAt FROM revisions WHERE document_id = ? AND 
revision_number = ?'
  ).get(documentId, revisionNumber);
}

function reportPayload(documentId) {
  const document = getDocument(documentId);
  if (!document) {
    return null;
  }
  return {
    document,
    revisions: getRevisionHistory(documentId),
    reports: listDocuments(),
  };
}

function validateSaveBody(body, routeDocumentId) {
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) {
    return { error: 'Request body must be a JSON object.', status: 400 };
  }

  const { baseRevision, content, documentId } = body;

  if (documentId !== undefined && documentId !== routeDocumentId) {
    return { error: 'documentId must match the requested report.', status: 400 
};
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(baseRevision)) {
    return { error: 'baseRevision must be an integer.', status: 400 };
  }
  if (typeof content !== 'string') {
    return { error: 'content must be a text string.', status: 400 };
  }

  return { baseRevision, content };
}

app.get('/api/reports', (req, res) => {
  res.json({ reports: listDocuments() });
});

app.get('/api/reports/:documentId', (req, res) => {
  const payload = reportPayload(req.params.documentId);
  if (!payload) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Unknown report.' });
    return;
  }
  res.json(payload);
});

app.get('/api/reports/:documentId/revisions', (req, res) => {
  const document = getDocument(req.params.documentId);
  if (!document) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Unknown report.' });
    return;
  }
  res.json({ documentId: document.id, revisions: getRevisionHistory(document.id)
});
});

app.get('/api/reports/:documentId/revisions/:revisionNumber', (req, res) => {
  const document = getDocument(req.params.documentId);
  if (!document) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Unknown report.' });
    return;
  }
  const revisionNumber = Number(req.params.revisionNumber);
  if (!Number.isInteger(revisionNumber)) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Revision number must be an integer.' });
    return;
  }
  const revision = getRevision(document.id, revisionNumber);
  if (!revision) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Unknown revision.' });
    return;
  }
  res.json({
    documentId: revision.documentId,
    revisionNumber: revision.revisionNumber,
    content: revision.content,
    createdAt: revision.createdAt,
  });
});

app.post('/api/reports/:documentId/save', (req, res) => {
  const validation = validateSaveBody(req.body, req.params.documentId);
  if (validation.error) {
    res.status(validation.status || 400).json({ error: validation.error });
    return;
  }

  const documentId = req.params.documentId;
  const document = getDocument(documentId);
  if (!document) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Unknown report.' });
    return;
  }

  if (validation.baseRevision !== document.currentRevision) {
    res.status(409).json({
      error: 'Save conflict: the report has changed on the server.',
      document: {
        id: document.id,
        currentRevision: document.currentRevision,
        content: document.content,
      },
    });
    return;
  }

  if (validation.content === document.content) {
    res.json({
      saved: true,
      changed: false,
      document: {
        id: document.id,
        currentRevision: document.currentRevision,
        updatedAt: document.updatedAt,
        content: document.content,
      },
    });
    return;
  }

  const nextRevision = document.currentRevision + 1;
  const now = isoNow();

  db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
  try {
    const lockedDocument = getDocument(documentId);
    if (!lockedDocument) {
      db.exec('ROLLBACK');
      res.status(404).json({ error: 'Unknown report.' });
      return;
    }
    if (lockedDocument.currentRevision !== validation.baseRevision) {
      db.exec('ROLLBACK');
      res.status(409).json({
        error: 'Save conflict: the report has changed on the server.',
        document: {
          id: lockedDocument.id,
          currentRevision: lockedDocument.currentRevision,
          content: lockedDocument.content,
        },
      });
      return;
    }

    db.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO revisions (document_id, revision_number, content, created_at)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)'
    ).run(documentId, nextRevision, validation.content, now);
    db.prepare(
      'UPDATE documents SET content = ?, current_revision = ?, updated_at = ? 
WHERE id = ?'
    ).run(validation.content, nextRevision, now, documentId);
    db.exec('COMMIT');
    res.json({
      saved: true,
      changed: true,
      document: {
        id: documentId,
        currentRevision: nextRevision,
        updatedAt: now,
        content: validation.content,
      },
    });
  } catch (error) {
    try {
      db.exec('ROLLBACK');
    } catch {
      // ignore rollback errors
    }
    res.status(500).json({ error: 'Unable to save the report.' });
  }
});

app.get('/', (req, res) => {
  const reports = listDocuments();
  const activeReportId = req.query.report && reports.some((report) => report.id 
=== req.query.report)
    ? String(req.query.report)
    : reports[0]?.id || null;

  res.type('html').send(`<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <title>PatchPad</title>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="appShell">
      <header class="app-header">
        <div>
          <div class="eyebrow">PatchPad</div>
          <h1>Incident report editor</h1>
          <p class="subtitle">Custom editing, revision history, and 
conflict-safe saving.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="report-list-card">
          <h2>Available reports</h2>
          <div id="report-list" data-active-report="${activeReportId || ''}">
            ${reports.map((report) => `
              <button type="button" class="report-chip${report.id === 
activeReportId ? ' active' : ''}" data-report-id="${report.id}">
                <strong>${escapeHtml(report.title)}</strong>
                <span>${escapeHtml(report.author)}</span>
                <small>Revision ${report.currentRevision}</small>
              </button>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>
      <main class="workspace">
        <section class="editor-panel">
          <div class="editor-toolbar">
            <div class="toolbar-group">
              <button type="button" id="saveButton">Save</button>
              <button type="button" id="reloadButton">Reload latest</button>
              <button type="button" id="discardButton">Discard draft</button>
            </div>
            <div class="toolbar-group">
              <button type="button" id="undoButton">Undo</button>
              <button type="button" id="redoButton">Redo</button>
            </div>
            <div class="toolbar-group">
              <button type="button" id="findToggleButton">Find</button>
              <button type="button" id="findPrevButton">Find Previous</button>
              <button type="button" id="findNextButton">Find Next</button>
              <button type="button" id="replaceCurrentButton">Replace 
Current</button>
              <button type="button" id="replaceAllButton">Replace All</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="find-bar" id="findBar" hidden>
            <label>
              Find
              <input id="findInput" type="text" autocomplete="off" 
spellcheck="false" placeholder="Search report text" />
            </label>
            <label>
              Replace
              <input id="replaceInput" type="text" autocomplete="off" 
spellcheck="false" placeholder="Replacement text" />
            </label>
            <div class="find-summary">
              <span id="findCount">0 matches</span>
              <span id="findStatus"></span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="document-title-block">
            <h2 id="documentTitle">Loading…</h2>

        <aside class="history-panel">
          <div class="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h2>Revision history</h2>
              <p>Preview or restore earlier saved revisions.</p>
            </div>

          <div id="historyList" class="history-list"></div>
        </aside>
      </main>
      <div id="previewModal" class="modal" hidden>
        <div class="modal-backdrop"></div>
        <div class="modal-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" 
aria-labelledby="previewTitle">
          <div class="modal-header">
            <div>
              <h3 id="previewTitle">Revision preview</h3>
              <p id="previewMeta"></p>
            </div>
            <button type="button" id="closePreviewButton">Close</button>

            <div class="line-gutter preview-gutter" id="previewGutter" 
aria-hidden="true"></div>
            <div class="editor-scroll preview-scroll" id="previewScroll"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <script>
      window.__PATCHPAD_BOOT__ = ${JSON.stringify({ reports, activeReportId })};
    </script>
    <script src="/client.js"></script>
  </body>
</html>`);
});

function escapeHtml(value) {
  return String(value)
    .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
    .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
    .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
    .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
    .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
}

  console.log(`PatchPad listening on 0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
