          state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
          finishGroup();
          queueRender();
          scrollSelectionIntoView();
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'End') {
          event.preventDefault();
          state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
            const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
            const target = mod ? state.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
            return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
          });
          state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
          finishGroup();
          queueRender();
          scrollSelectionIntoView();
          return;
        }
        if (mod && key === 'g') {
          event.preventDefault();
          navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
          return;
        }
        if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
          event.preventDefault();
          typeText(key);
        }
      }
    
      function handlePaste(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        typeText(event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '');
      }
    
      function handleCopy(event) {
        const range = getPrimaryRange();
        if (range.start === range.end) {
          return;
        }
        event.preventDefault();
        event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
      }
    
      function handleCut(event) {
        const range = getPrimaryRange();
        if (range.start === range.end) {
          return;
        }
        event.preventDefault();
        event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
        applyReplacement([range], '', 'cut');
      }
    
      function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
        if (event.key === 'Escape') {
          event.preventDefault();
          editorScroll.focus();
          return;
        }
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
          event.preventDefault();
          navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
        }
      }
    
      function handleScroll() {
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function handlePreviewScroll() {
        previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function handleWindowResize() {
        metricsCache.clear();
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function bindUi() {
        editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
        window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
        previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
        findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
          state.findQuery = findInput.value;
          state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
          findMatches();
        });
        findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
        replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
          if (event.key === 'Escape') {
            editorScroll.focus();
          }
        });
        findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
          findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
          if (!findBar.hidden) {
            findInput.focus();
            findInput.select();
          } else {
            editorScroll.focus();
          }
        });
        findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
        findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
        replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
        replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
        saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
        reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
        discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
        undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
        redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
        closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
        previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
          if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
            closePreview();
          }
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
      }
    
      function closePreview() {
        state.preview = null;
        previewModal.hidden = true;
        editorScroll.focus();
      }
    
      function reloadLatest() {
        if (state.document) {
          loadReport(state.document.id);
        }
      }
    
      function handleUndo() {
        if (!state.undoStack.length) {
          setMessage('Nothing to undo.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const current = snapshot();
        const previous = state.undoStack.pop();
        state.redoStack.push(current);
        restoreSnapshot(previous);
        setDirtyStatus();
        setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
      }
    
      function handleRedo() {
        if (!state.redoStack.length) {
          setMessage('Nothing to redo.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const current = snapshot();
        const next = state.redoStack.pop();
        state.undoStack.push(current);
        restoreSnapshot(next);
        setDirtyStatus();
        setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
      }
    
      function insertTab(shiftKey) {
        const range = getPrimaryRange();
        if (range.start !== range.end && state.content.slice(range.start, 
range.end).includes('\n')) {
          indentSelection(shiftKey);
          return;
        }
        if (shiftKey) {
          indentSelection(true);
          return;
        }
        typeText('\t');
      }
    
      function openFindBar() {
        findBar.hidden = false;
        findInput.focus();
        findInput.select();
      }
    
      function loadReport(reportId) {
        if (!reportId) {
          return Promise.resolve();
        }
        setStatus('Loading report…');
        setMessage('');
        return fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`)
          .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
          .then(({ response, data }) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load report.');
            }
            state.reportId = data.document.id;
            state.document = data.document;
            state.revisions = data.revisions;
            state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
            state.savedContent = data.document.content;
            state.conflict = null;
            state.undoStack = [];
            state.redoStack = [];
            state.typingGroup = null;
            state.findQuery = '';
            findInput.value = '';
            state.matches = [];
            state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
            updateContentModel(data.document.content);
            state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
            renderHeader();
            renderReports();
            renderHistory();
            renderFindBar();
            setDirtyStatus();
            queueRender();
            setMessage('Report loaded.', 'good');
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/$
{encodeURIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
          .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
          .then(({ response, data }) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
            }
            state.preview = data;
            previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
            previewModal.hidden = false;
            previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
            previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
            renderPreview();
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/$
{encodeURIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
          .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
          .then(({ response, data }) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
            }
            const before = snapshot();
            pushUndo(before, 'restore');
            updateContentModel(data.content);
            state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
            state.matches = [];
            state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
            renderFindBar();
            queueRender();
            setDirtyStatus();
            setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function renderReports() {
        Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip')).forEach((chip) 
=> {
          chip.classList.toggle('active', chip.dataset.reportId === 
state.reportId);
          chip.onclick = () => loadReport(chip.dataset.reportId);
        });
      }
    
      function renderHeader() {
        if (!state.document) {
          documentTitle.textContent = 'Loading…';
          documentMeta.textContent = '';
          return;
        }
        documentTitle.textContent = state.document.title;
        documentMeta.textContent = `Author: ${state.document.author} • 
${state.document.summary}`;
      }
    
      function renderHistory() {
        if (!state.revisions.length) {
          historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
          return;
        }
        historyList.innerHTML = state.revisions.map((revision) => `
          <article class="history-entry">
            <div>
              <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
              <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
            </div>
            <div class="history-entry-actions">
              <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
              <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
            </div>
          </article>
        `).join('');
        historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]').forEach((button)
=> {
          button.onclick = () => openPreview(button.dataset.previewRevision);
        });
        historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]').forEach((button)
=> {
          button.onclick = () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision);
        });
      }
    
      function renderFindBar() {
        findCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
match${state.matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
        if (!state.findQuery) {
          findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
        } else if (!state.matches.length) {
          findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
        } else {
          findStatus.textContent = `Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${state.matches.length}`;
        }
      }
    
      function setDirtyStatus() {
        if (state.conflict) {
          setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${state.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
          return;
        }
        if (state.content === state.savedContent) {
          setStatus(`Saved at revision ${state.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
        } else {
          setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${state.baseRevision}`, 
'warning');
        }
      }
    
      function renderStatus() {
        setDirtyStatus();
        const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
        const column = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
        cursorState.textContent = state.selectionRanges.length > 1
          ? `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1} • 
${state.selectionRanges.length} carets`
          : `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1}`;
      }
    
      function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, content, 
mode) {
        const rowHeight = state.rowHeight;
        const scrollTop = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.top : 
scrollContainer.scrollTop;
        const scrollLeft = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.left : 
scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
        const visibleHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
        const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 2);
        const endLine = Math.min(content.lines.length - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop 
+ visibleHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
        const totalHeight = content.lines.length * rowHeight;
        const ranges = content.selectionRanges || [];
        const matches = content.matches || [];
        const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;
    
        const rows = [];
        const gutterRows = [];
        let maxWidth = 0;
        for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 1) {
          const line = content.lines[lineIndex] || '';
          const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] || 0;
          const lineWidth = ensureLineMetrics(line).width;
          maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, lineWidth);
          const top = lineIndex * rowHeight;
          const selectionRects = [];
          const matchRects = [];
          const carets = [];
    
          for (const range of ranges) {
            const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
            const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + line.length);
            if (start < end) {
              const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
              selectionRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end
- lineStart) - left) });
            }
            if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && 
range.start <= lineStart + line.length) {
              carets.push({ left: offsetToX(line, range.start - lineStart) });
            }
          }
    
          for (let index = 0; index < matches.length; index += 1) {
            const match = matches[index];
            const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
            const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + line.length);
            if (start < end) {
              const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
              matchRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end - 
lineStart) - left), current: index === activeMatchIndex });
            }
          }
    
          rows.push(`
            <div class="line-row" style="top:${top}px; height:${rowHeight}px;">
              ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div class="match-rect${rect.current ?
' current-match' : ''}" style="left:${rect.left}px; 
width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
              ${selectionRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
              ${carets.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
              <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>
            </div>
          `);
          gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
        }
    
        scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px; min-width:${maxWidth + 
200}px;">${rows.join('')}</div>`;
        gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
        scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
        scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
      }
    
      function renderEditor() {
        if (!state.document) {
          return;
        }
        renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
          lines: state.lines,
          lineStarts: state.lineStarts,
          selectionRanges: state.selectionRanges,
          matches: state.matches,
          activeMatchIndex: state.activeMatchIndex,
        }, 'editor');
        renderStatus();
      }
    
      function renderPreview() {
        if (!state.preview) {
          return;
        }
        const lines = state.preview.content.split('\n');
        const lineStarts = [0];
        for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
          lineStarts[index + 1] = lineStarts[index] + lines[index].length + 
(index < lines.length - 1 ? 1 : 0);
        }
        renderEditorSurface(previewScroll, previewGutter, {
          lines,
          lineStarts,
          selectionRanges: [],
          matches: [],
          activeMatchIndex: -1,
        }, 'preview');
      }
    
      function queueRender() {
        if (renderQueued) {
          return;
        }
        renderQueued = true;
        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          renderQueued = false;
          renderEditor();
          if (state.preview) {
            renderPreview();
          }
        });
      }
    
      function scrollSelectionIntoView() {
        const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
        const caretX = offsetToX(info.line, info.columnOffset);
        const caretY = info.lineIndex * state.rowHeight;
        if (caretY < editorScroll.scrollTop + 16) {
          editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - state.rowHeight * 3);
        } else if (caretY + state.rowHeight > editorScroll.scrollTop + 
editorScroll.clientHeight - 16) {
          editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - 
editorScroll.clientHeight + state.rowHeight * 4);
        }
        if (caretX < editorScroll.scrollLeft + 24) {
          editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 40);
        } else if (caretX > editorScroll.scrollLeft + editorScroll.clientWidth -
24) {
          editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 
editorScroll.clientWidth + 80);
        }
      }
    
      function handleEditorMouseDown(event) {
        const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
        const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
        const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
        const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
        const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, Math.max(0,
x));
    
        if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
          state.selectionRanges = [...state.selectionRanges, { anchor: offset, 
head: offset }];
          finishGroup();
          queueRender();
          return;
        }
    
        if (event.detail >= 3) {
          state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex], head: 
state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + line.length }];
        } else if (event.detail === 2 && segmenterWord) {
          const relative = offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex];
          const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
          let selected = { start: 0, end: line.length };
          for (const segment of segments) {
            const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
            if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index <= relative && relative <= 
end) {
              selected = { start: segment.index, end };
              break;
            }
          }
          state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + 
selected.start, head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + selected.end }];
        } else {
          state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: offset, head: offset }];
          dragSelection = { start: offset, active: true };
        }
        finishGroup();
        queueRender();
        scrollSelectionIntoView();
      }
    
      function handleEditorMouseMove(event) {
        if (!dragSelection || !dragSelection.active) {
          return;
        }
        const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
        const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
        const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
        const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
        const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, Math.max(0,
x));
        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: dragSelection.start, head: offset }];
        queueRender();
        scrollSelectionIntoView();
      }
    
      function finishDragging() {
        dragSelection = null;
      }
    
      function handleEditorShortcut(event) {
        const isMac = navigator.platform.toLowerCase().includes('mac');
        const mod = isMac ? event.metaKey : event.ctrlKey;
        const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
    
        if (mod && key === 'f') {
          event.preventDefault();
          openFindBar();
          return true;
        }
        if (mod && key === 's') {
          event.preventDefault();
          saveReport();
          return true;
        }
        if (mod && key === 'z' && !event.shiftKey) {
          event.preventDefault();
          handleUndo();
          return true;
        }
        if (mod && (key === 'y' || (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey))) {
          event.preventDefault();
          handleRedo();
          return true;
        }
        if (mod && key === 'a') {
          event.preventDefault();
          selectAll();
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      }
    
      function handleEditorKeyDown(event) {
        if (handleEditorShortcut(event)) {
          return;
        }
    
        const mod = event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey;
        const shift = event.shiftKey;
        const key = event.key;
    
        if (key === 'Escape') {
          event.preventDefault();
          findInput.focus();
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'Tab') {
          event.preventDefault();
          insertTab(shift);
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'Enter') {
          event.preventDefault();
          typeText('\n');
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'Backspace') {
          event.preventDefault();
          deleteByDirection(-1);
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'Delete') {
          event.preventDefault();
          deleteByDirection(1);
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'ArrowLeft') {
          event.preventDefault();
          moveRanges(-1, { shift, byWord: mod });
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'ArrowRight') {
          event.preventDefault();
          moveRanges(1, { shift, byWord: mod });
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'ArrowUp') {
          event.preventDefault();
          moveVertical(-1, shift);
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'ArrowDown') {
          event.preventDefault();
          moveVertical(1, shift);
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'Home') {
          event.preventDefault();
          state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
            const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
            const target = mod ? 0 : info.lineStart;
            return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
          });
          state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
          finishGroup();
          queueRender();
          scrollSelectionIntoView();
          return;
        }
        if (key === 'End') {
          event.preventDefault();
          state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
            const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
            const target = mod ? state.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
            return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
          });
          state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
          finishGroup();
          queueRender();
          scrollSelectionIntoView();
          return;
        }
        if (mod && key === 'g') {
          event.preventDefault();
          navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
          return;
        }
        if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
          event.preventDefault();
          typeText(key);
        }
      }
    
      function handlePaste(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        typeText(event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '');
      }
    
      function handleCopy(event) {
        const range = getPrimaryRange();
        if (range.start === range.end) {
          return;
        }
        event.preventDefault();
        event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
      }
    
      function handleCut(event) {
        const range = getPrimaryRange();
        if (range.start === range.end) {
          return;
        }
        event.preventDefault();
        event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
        applyReplacement([range], '', 'cut');
      }
    
      function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
        if (event.key === 'Escape') {
          event.preventDefault();
          editorScroll.focus();
          return;
        }
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
          event.preventDefault();
          navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
        }
      }
    
      function handleScroll() {
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function handlePreviewScroll() {
        previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function handleWindowResize() {
        metricsCache.clear();
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function bindUi() {
        editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
        window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
        previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
        findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
          state.findQuery = findInput.value;
          state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
          findMatches();
        });
        findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
        replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
          if (event.key === 'Escape') {
            editorScroll.focus();
          }
        });
        findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
          findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
          if (!findBar.hidden) {
            findInput.focus();
            findInput.select();
          } else {
            editorScroll.focus();
          }
        });
        findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
        findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
        replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
        replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
        saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
        reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
        discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
        undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
        redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
        closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
        previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
          if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
            closePreview();
          }
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
      }
    
      function closePreview() {
        state.preview = null;
        previewModal.hidden = true;
        editorScroll.focus();
      }
    
      function reloadLatest() {
        if (state.document) {
          loadReport(state.document.id);
        }
      }
    
      function handleUndo() {
        if (!state.undoStack.length) {
          setMessage('Nothing to undo.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const current = snapshot();
        const previous = state.undoStack.pop();
        state.redoStack.push(current);
        restoreSnapshot(previous);
        setDirtyStatus();
        setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
      }
    
      function handleRedo() {
        if (!state.redoStack.length) {
          setMessage('Nothing to redo.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const current = snapshot();
        const next = state.redoStack.pop();
        state.undoStack.push(current);
        restoreSnapshot(next);
        setDirtyStatus();
        setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
      }
    
      function insertTab(shiftKey) {
        const range = getPrimaryRange();
        if (range.start !== range.end && state.content.slice(range.start, 
range.end).includes('\n')) {
          indentSelection(shiftKey);
          return;
        }
        if (shiftKey) {
          indentSelection(true);
          return;
        }
        typeText('\t');
      }
    
      function openFindBar() {
        findBar.hidden = false;
        findInput.focus();
        findInput.select();
      }
    
      function loadReport(reportId) {
        if (!reportId) {
          return Promise.resolve();
        }
        setStatus('Loading report…');
        setMessage('');
        return fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`)
          .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
          .then(({ response, data }) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load report.');
            }
            state.reportId = data.document.id;
            state.document = data.document;
            state.revisions = data.revisions;
            state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
            state.savedContent = data.document.content;
            state.conflict = null;
            state.undoStack = [];
            state.redoStack = [];
            state.typingGroup = null;
            state.findQuery = '';
            findInput.value = '';
            state.matches = [];
            state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
            updateContentModel(data.document.content);
            state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
            renderHeader();
            renderReports();
            renderHistory();
            renderFindBar();
            setDirtyStatus();
            queueRender();
            setMessage('Report loaded.', 'good');
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/$
{encodeURIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
          .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
          .then(({ response, data }) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
            }
            state.preview = data;
            previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
            previewModal.hidden = false;
            previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
            previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
            renderPreview();
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/$
{encodeURIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
          .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
          .then(({ response, data }) => {
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
            }
            const before = snapshot();
            pushUndo(before, 'restore');
            updateContentModel(data.content);
            state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
            state.matches = [];
            state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
            renderFindBar();
            queueRender();
            setDirtyStatus();
            setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function saveReport() {
        if (!state.document) {
          return;
        }
        setMessage('Saving report…');
        fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/save`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ documentId: state.document.id, baseRevision: 
state.baseRevision, content: state.content }),
        })
          .then(async (response) => {
            const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
            if (response.status === 409) {
              state.conflict = data.document || { currentRevision: 'unknown' };
              setDirtyStatus();
              setMessage('Save conflict: another tab saved a newer version. Your
draft is preserved.', 'warning');
              return null;
            }
            if (!response.ok) {
              throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed.');
            }
            return data;
          })
          .then((data) => {
            if (!data) {
              return;
            }
            state.conflict = null;
            state.savedContent = data.document.content;
            state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
            state.document.currentRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
            state.document.updatedAt = data.document.updatedAt;
            renderReports();
            renderHistory();
            setDirtyStatus();
            setMessage(data.changed ? `Saved as revision ${state.baseRevision}.`
: 'No changes to save.', 'good');
          })
          .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
      }
    
      function replaceCurrentMatch() {
        if (!state.matches.length) {
          setMessage('No match to replace.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const match = state.matches[state.activeMatchIndex < 0 ? 0 : 
state.activeMatchIndex];
        const before = snapshot();
        pushUndo(before, 'replace-current');
        updateContentModel(state.content.slice(0, match.start) + 
replaceInput.value + state.content.slice(match.end));
        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start + 
replaceInput.value.length, head: match.start + replaceInput.value.length }];
        findMatches();
        queueRender();
        setDirtyStatus();
        setMessage('Replaced the current match.', 'good');
      }
    
      function replaceAllMatches() {
        if (!state.matches.length) {
          setMessage('No matches to replace.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const before = snapshot();
        pushUndo(before, 'replace-all');
        const replacement = replaceInput.value;
        let content = state.content;
        for (let index = state.matches.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
          const match = state.matches[index];
          content = content.slice(0, match.start) + replacement + 
content.slice(match.end);
        }
        updateContentModel(content);
        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
        findMatches();
        queueRender();
        setDirtyStatus();
        setMessage(`Replaced all ${before.matches.length} matches.`, 'good');
      }
    
      function openFindBar() {
        findBar.hidden = false;
        findInput.focus();
        findInput.select();
      }
    
      function loadInitialReport() {
        if (state.reportId) {
          return loadReport(state.reportId);
        }
        if (boot.activeReportId) {
          return loadReport(boot.activeReportId);
        }
        return Promise.resolve();
      }
    
      function handleFindSelection() {
        if (!state.findQuery) {
          state.matches = [];
          state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
          renderFindBar();
          queueRender();
          return;
        }
        const matches = [];
        let index = state.content.indexOf(state.findQuery);
        while (index !== -1) {
          matches.push({ start: index, end: index + state.findQuery.length });
          index = state.content.indexOf(state.findQuery, index + Math.max(1, 
state.findQuery.length));
        }
        state.matches = matches;
        if (!matches.length) {
          state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
        } else if (state.activeMatchIndex < 0 || state.activeMatchIndex >= 
matches.length) {
          state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
        }
        renderFindBar();
        if (matches.length) {
          const match = matches[state.activeMatchIndex];
          state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }];
          scrollSelectionIntoView();
        }
        queueRender();
      }
    
      function navigateMatches(direction) {
        if (!state.matches.length) {
          setMessage('No matches found.', 'warning');
          return;
        }
        const total = state.matches.length;
        state.activeMatchIndex = (state.activeMatchIndex + direction + total) % 
total;
        const match = state.matches[state.activeMatchIndex];
        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }];
        renderFindBar();
        scrollSelectionIntoView();
        queueRender();
        setMessage(`Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of ${total}.`, 'good');
      }
    
      function findMatches() {
        handleFindSelection();
      }
    
      function bindData() {
        editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
        window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
        editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
        previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
        findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
          state.findQuery = findInput.value;
          state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
          findMatches();
        });
        findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
        replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
          if (event.key === 'Escape') {
            editorScroll.focus();
          }
        });
        findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
          findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
          if (!findBar.hidden) {
            findInput.focus();
            findInput.select();
          } else {
            editorScroll.focus();
          }
        });
        findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
        findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
        replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
        replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
        saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
        reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
        discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
        undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
        redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
        closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
        previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
          if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
            closePreview();
          }
        });
        window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
      }
    
      function init() {
        renderReports();
        bindData();
        loadInitialReport().then(() => {
          editorScroll.focus();
          queueRender();
        });
      }
    
      init();
    })();

+86|  function normalizedRange(range) {
+87|    return range.anchor <= range.head
+88|      ? { start: range.anchor, end: range.head, anchor: range.anchor, head:

+89|      : { start: range.head, end: range.anchor, anchor: range.anchor, head:

+92|  function sortRanges(ranges) {
+93|    return ranges.map(normalizedRange).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start ||

+96|  function dedupeCollapsed(ranges) {
+97|    const seen = new Set();
+98|    const result = [];
+99|    for (const range of ranges) {
+100|      if (range.start === range.end) {
+101|        const key = String(range.start);
+102|        if (seen.has(key)) {
+103|          continue;
+104|        }
+105|        seen.add(key);
+106|      }
+107|      result.push(range);
+108|    }
+109|    return result;

+192|    }
+193|    measureContext.font = getComputedStyle(editorScroll).font;
+194|    const offsets = [0];
+195|    const widths = [0];
+196|    let offset = 0;
+197|    let width = 0;
+198|    const segments = segmenterGrapheme ? 
Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(line), (part) => part.segment) : 
Array.from(line);
+199|    for (const segment of segments) {
+200|      offset += segment.length;
+201|      const display = segment === '\t' ? '    ' : segment;
+202|      width += measureContext.measureText(display).width;
+203|      offsets.push(offset);
+204|      widths.push(width);
+205|    }
+206|    const metrics = { offsets, widths, width };
+207|    metricsCache.set(line, metrics);
+208|    return metrics;
+209|  }
+210|
+211|  function offsetToX(line, offset) {
+212|    const metrics = ensureLineMetrics(line);
+213|    let low = 0;
+214|    let high = metrics.offsets.length - 1;
+215|    while (low <= high) {
+216|      const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
+217|      if (metrics.offsets[mid] === offset) {
+218|        return metrics.widths[mid];
+219|      }
+220|      if (metrics.offsets[mid] < offset) {
+221|        low = mid + 1;
+222|      } else {
+223|        high = mid - 1;
+224|      }
+225|    }
+226|    return metrics.widths[Math.max(0, high)];
+227|  }
+228|
+229|  function xToOffset(line, x) {
+230|    const metrics = ensureLineMetrics(line);
+231|    let low = 0;
+232|    let high = metrics.widths.length - 1;
+233|    while (low <= high) {
+234|      const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
+235|      if (metrics.widths[mid] === x) {
+236|        return metrics.offsets[mid];
+237|      }
+238|      if (metrics.widths[mid] < x) {
+239|        low = mid + 1;
+240|      } else {
+241|        high = mid - 1;
+242|      }
+243|    }
+244|    return metrics.offsets[Math.max(0, high)];
+245|  }
+246|
+247|  function lineInfoForOffset(offset) {
+248|    offset = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.content.length, offset));
+249|    let lineIndex = 0;
+250|    for (let index = state.lineStarts.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+251|      if (state.lineStarts[index] <= offset) {
+252|        lineIndex = Math.min(index, state.lines.length - 1);
+253|        break;
+254|      }
+255|    }
+256|    return {
+257|      lineIndex,
+258|      lineStart: state.lineStarts[lineIndex],
+259|      line: state.lines[lineIndex] || '',
+260|      columnOffset: offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex],
+261|    };
+262|  }
+263|
+264|  function nextGrapheme(line, offset, direction) {
+265|    if (direction > 0) {
+266|      if (offset >= line.length) {
+267|        return line.length;
+268|      }
+269|      const rest = line.slice(offset);
+270|      const segments = segmenterGrapheme ? 
Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(rest), (part) => part.segment) : 
Array.from(rest);
+271|      return offset + (segments[0]?.length || 1);
+272|    }
+273|    if (offset <= 0) {
+274|      return 0;
+275|    }
+276|    const part = line.slice(0, offset);
+277|    const segments = segmenterGrapheme ? 
Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(part), (seg) => seg.segment) : 
Array.from(part);
+278|    return offset - (segments[segments.length - 1]?.length || 1);
+279|  }
+280|
+281|  function nextWordBoundary(line, offset, direction) {
+282|    if (!segmenterWord) {
+283|      return nextGrapheme(line, offset, direction);
+284|    }
+285|    const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
+286|    if (direction > 0) {
+287|      for (const segment of segments) {
+288|        const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
+289|        if (segment.index > offset) {
+290|          return segment.index;
+291|        }
+292|        if (segment.index <= offset && offset < end) {
+293|          return end;
+294|        }
+295|      }
+296|      return line.length;
+297|    }
+298|    for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+299|      const segment = segments[index];
+300|      if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index < offset) {
+301|        return segment.index;
+302|      }
+303|    }
+304|    return 0;
+305|  }
+306|
+307|  function selectAll() {
+308|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: state.content.length }];
+309|    finishGroup();
+310|    queueRender();
+311|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+312|  }
+313|
+314|  function applyReplacement(ranges, replacement, kind) {
+315|    const sorted = sortRanges(ranges);
+316|    if (!sorted.length) {
+317|      return;
+318|    }
+319|    const before = snapshot();
+320|    pushUndo(before, kind);
+321|    let content = state.content;
+322|    const nextSelections = [];
+323|    for (let index = sorted.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+324|      const range = sorted[index];
+325|      content = content.slice(0, range.start) + replacement + 
content.slice(range.end);
+326|      nextSelections.unshift({ anchor: range.start + replacement.length, 
head: range.start + replacement.length });
+327|    }
+328|    updateContentModel(content);
+329|    state.selectionRanges = nextSelections;
+330|    state.matches = [];
+331|    state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+332|    renderFindBar();
+333|    queueRender();
+334|  }
+335|
+336|  function typeText(text) {
+337|    applyReplacement(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges), text, 'insert');
+338|  }
+339|
+340|  function deleteByDirection(direction) {
+341|    const ranges = sortRanges(state.selectionRanges).map((range) => {
+342|      if (range.start !== range.end) {
+343|        return range;
+344|      }
+345|      const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.start);
+346|      const relative = range.start - info.lineStart;
+347|      const line = info.line;
+348|      const nextRelative = direction < 0 ? nextGrapheme(line, relative, -1)
: nextGrapheme(line, relative, 1);
+349|      return direction < 0
+350|        ? { start: info.lineStart + nextRelative, end: range.start, anchor:
range.start, head: range.start }
+351|        : { start: range.start, end: info.lineStart + nextRelative, anchor:
range.start, head: range.start };
+352|    });
+353|    applyReplacement(ranges, '', direction < 0 ? 'backspace' : 'delete');
+354|  }
+355|
+356|  function moveRanges(direction, { shift = false, byWord = false } = {}) {
+357|    state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+358|      const normalized = normalizedRange(range);
+359|      const collapseTarget = shift ? range.head : (direction > 0 ? 
normalized.end : normalized.start);
+360|      const info = lineInfoForOffset(collapseTarget);
+361|      const relative = collapseTarget - info.lineStart;
+362|      const nextRelative = byWord ? nextWordBoundary(info.line, relative, 
direction) : nextGrapheme(info.line, relative, direction);
+363|      const nextOffset = info.lineStart + nextRelative;
+364|      return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: nextOffset } : { anchor:
nextOffset, head: nextOffset };
+365|    });
+366|    state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+367|    finishGroup();
+368|    queueRender();
+369|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+370|  }
+371|
+372|  function moveVertical(direction, shift = false) {
+373|    state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+374|      const current = shift ? range.head : normalizedRange(range).end;
+375|      const info = lineInfoForOffset(current);
+376|      const nextLine = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
info.lineIndex + direction));
+377|      const line = state.lines[nextLine] || '';
+378|      const nextColumn = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
+379|      const nextMetrics = ensureLineMetrics(line);
+380|      const nextRelative = nextMetrics.offsets[Math.min(nextColumn, 
nextMetrics.offsets.length - 1)];
+381|      const nextOffset = state.lineStarts[nextLine] + nextRelative;
+382|      return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: nextOffset } : { anchor:
nextOffset, head: nextOffset };
+383|    });
+384|    state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+385|    finishGroup();
+386|    queueRender();
+387|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+388|  }
+389|
+390|  function currentSelectionText() {
+391|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+392|    return state.content.slice(range.start, range.end);
+393|  }
+394|
+395|  function indentSelection(outdent = false) {
+396|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+397|    const startLine = lineInfoForOffset(range.start).lineIndex;
+398|    const endLine = lineInfoForOffset(range.end).lineIndex;
+399|    const lines = state.lines.slice(startLine, endLine + 1);
+400|    if (!lines.length) {
+401|      return;
+402|    }
+403|    const before = snapshot();
+404|    pushUndo(before, outdent ? 'outdent' : 'indent');
+405|    const adjusted = lines.map((line) => {
+406|      if (outdent) {
+407|        if (line.startsWith('\t')) {
+408|          return line.slice(1);
+409|        }
+410|        if (line.startsWith('    ')) {
+411|          return line.slice(4);
+412|        }
+413|        if (line.startsWith(' ')) {
+414|          return line.replace(/^ +/, (match) => match.slice(Math.min(4, 
match.length)));
+415|        }
+416|        return line;
+417|      }
+418|      return `\t${line}`;
+419|    });
+420|    const startOffset = state.lineStarts[startLine];
+421|    const endOffset = endLine < state.lines.length - 1 ? 
state.lineStarts[endLine + 1] : state.content.length;
+422|    updateContentModel(state.content.slice(0, startOffset) + 
adjusted.join('\n') + state.content.slice(endOffset));
+423|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: startOffset, head: startOffset + 
adjusted.join('\n').length }];
+424|    queueRender();
+425|    setDirtyStatus();
+426|  }
+427|
+428|  function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
+429|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+430|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
+431|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+432|        if (!response.ok) {
+433|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
+434|        }
+435|        state.preview = data;
+436|        previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
+437|        previewModal.hidden = false;
+438|        previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
+439|        previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
+440|        renderPreview();
+441|      })
+442|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+443|  }
+444|
+445|  function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
+446|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+447|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data 
})))
+448|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+449|        if (!response.ok) {
+450|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
+451|        }
+452|        const before = snapshot();
+453|        pushUndo(before, 'restore');
+454|        updateContentModel(data.content);
+455|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+456|        state.matches = [];
+457|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+458|        renderFindBar();
+459|        queueRender();
+460|        setDirtyStatus();
+461|        setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
+462|      })
+463|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+464|  }
+465|
+466|  function renderReports() {
+467|    Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip')).forEach((chip) 
=> {
+468|      chip.classList.toggle('active', chip.dataset.reportId === 
state.reportId);
+469|      chip.onclick = () => loadReport(chip.dataset.reportId);
+470|    });
+471|  }

+473|  function renderHeader() {
+474|    if (!state.document) {
+475|      documentTitle.textContent = 'Loading…';
+476|      documentMeta.textContent = '';
+477|      return;
+478|    }
+479|    documentTitle.textContent = state.document.title;
+480|    documentMeta.textContent = `Author: ${state.document.author} • 
${state.document.summary}`;
+481|  }
