+483|  function renderHistory() {
+484|    if (!state.revisions.length) {
+485|      historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
+486|      return;
+487|    }
+488|    historyList.innerHTML = state.revisions.map((revision) => `
+489|      <article class="history-entry">
+490|        <div>
+491|          <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
+492|          <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
+493|        </div>
+494|        <div class="history-entry-actions">
+495|          <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
+496|          <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
+497|        </div>
+498|      </article>
+499|    `).join('');
+500|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+501|      button.onclick = () => openPreview(button.dataset.previewRevision);
+502|    });
+503|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+504|      button.onclick = () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision);
+505|    });
+506|  }
+507|
+508|  function renderFindBar() {
+509|    findCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
match${state.matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
+510|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+511|      findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
+512|    } else if (!state.matches.length) {
+513|      findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
+514|    } else {
+515|      findStatus.textContent = `Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${state.matches.length}`;
+516|    }
+517|  }
+518|
+519|  function setDirtyStatus() {
+520|    if (state.conflict) {
+521|      setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${state.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
+522|      return;
+523|    }
+524|    if (state.content === state.savedContent) {
+525|      setStatus(`Saved at revision ${state.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
+526|    } else {
+527|      setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${state.baseRevision}`, 
'warning');
+528|    }
+529|  }
+530|
+531|  function renderStatus() {
+532|    setDirtyStatus();
+533|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+534|    const column = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
+535|    cursorState.textContent = state.selectionRanges.length > 1
+536|      ? `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1} • 
${state.selectionRanges.length} carets`
+537|      : `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1}`;
+538|  }
+539|
+540|  function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, content, 
mode) {
+541|    const rowHeight = state.rowHeight;
+542|    const scrollTop = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.top : 
scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+543|    const scrollLeft = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.left : 
scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
+544|    const visibleHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
+545|    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 2);
+546|    const endLine = Math.min(content.lines.length - 1, Math.ceil((scrollTop
+ visibleHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
+547|    const totalHeight = content.lines.length * rowHeight;
+548|    const ranges = content.selectionRanges || [];
+549|    const matches = content.matches || [];
+550|    const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;
+551|
+552|    const rows = [];
+553|    const gutterRows = [];
+554|    let maxWidth = 0;
+555|    for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 1) {
+556|      const line = content.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+557|      const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] || 0;
+558|      const lineWidth = ensureLineMetrics(line).width;
+559|      maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, lineWidth);
+560|      const top = lineIndex * rowHeight;
+561|      const selectionRects = [];
+562|      const matchRects = [];
+563|      const carets = [];
+564|
+565|      for (const range of ranges) {
+566|        const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
+567|        const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + line.length);
+568|        if (start < end) {
+569|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+570|          selectionRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, 
end - lineStart) - left) });
+571|        }
+572|        if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && 
range.start <= lineStart + line.length) {
+573|          carets.push({ left: offsetToX(line, range.start - lineStart) });
+574|        }
+575|      }
+576|
+577|      for (let index = 0; index < matches.length; index += 1) {
+578|        const match = matches[index];
+579|        const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
+580|        const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + line.length);
+581|        if (start < end) {
+582|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+583|          matchRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end - 
lineStart) - left), current: index === activeMatchIndex });
+584|        }
+585|      }
+586|
+587|      rows.push(`
+588|        <div class="line-row" style="top:${top}px; height:${rowHeight}px;">
+589|          ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div class="match-rect${rect.current 
? ' current-match' : ''}" style="left:${rect.left}px; 
width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+590|          ${selectionRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+591|          ${carets.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+592|          <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>
+593|        </div>
+594|      `);
+595|      gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
+596|    }
+597|
+598|    scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px; min-width:${maxWidth + 
200}px;">${rows.join('')}</div>`;
+599|    gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
+600|    scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
+601|    scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
+602|  }
+603|
+604|  function renderEditor() {
+605|    if (!state.document) {
+606|      return;
+607|    }
+608|    renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
+609|      lines: state.lines,
+610|      lineStarts: state.lineStarts,
+611|      selectionRanges: state.selectionRanges,
+612|      matches: state.matches,
+613|      activeMatchIndex: state.activeMatchIndex,
+614|    }, 'editor');
+615|    renderStatus();
+616|  }
+617|
+618|  function renderPreview() {
+619|    if (!state.preview) {
+620|      return;
+621|    }
+622|    const lines = state.preview.content.split('\n');
+623|    const lineStarts = [0];
+624|    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
+625|      lineStarts[index + 1] = lineStarts[index] + lines[index].length + 
(index < lines.length - 1 ? 1 : 0);
+626|    }
+627|    renderEditorSurface(previewScroll, previewGutter, {
+628|      lines,
+629|      lineStarts,
+630|      selectionRanges: [],
+631|      matches: [],
+632|      activeMatchIndex: -1,
+633|    }, 'preview');
+634|  }
+635|
+636|  function queueRender() {
+637|    if (renderQueued) {
+638|      return;
+639|    }
+640|    renderQueued = true;
+641|    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+642|      renderQueued = false;
+643|      renderEditor();
+644|      if (state.preview) {
+645|        renderPreview();
+646|      }
+647|    });
+648|  }
+649|
+650|  function scrollSelectionIntoView() {
+651|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+652|    const caretX = offsetToX(info.line, info.columnOffset);
+653|    const caretY = info.lineIndex * state.rowHeight;
+654|    if (caretY < editorScroll.scrollTop + 16) {
+655|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - state.rowHeight * 3);
+656|    } else if (caretY + state.rowHeight > editorScroll.scrollTop + 
editorScroll.clientHeight - 16) {
+657|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - 
editorScroll.clientHeight + state.rowHeight * 4);
+658|    }
+659|    if (caretX < editorScroll.scrollLeft + 24) {
+660|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 40);
+661|    } else if (caretX > editorScroll.scrollLeft + editorScroll.clientWidth 
- 24) {
+662|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 
editorScroll.clientWidth + 80);
+663|    }
+664|  }
+665|
+666|  function handleEditorMouseDown(event) {
+667|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+668|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+669|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+670|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+671|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+672|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+673|
+674|    if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
+675|      state.selectionRanges = [...state.selectionRanges, { anchor: offset, 
head: offset }];
+676|      finishGroup();
+677|      queueRender();
+678|      return;
+679|    }
+680|
+681|    if (event.detail >= 3) {
+682|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex], head:
state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + line.length }];
+683|    } else if (event.detail === 2 && segmenterWord) {
+684|      const relative = offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex];
+685|      const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
+686|      let selected = { start: 0, end: line.length };
+687|      for (const segment of segments) {
+688|        const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
+689|        if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index <= relative && relative <= 
end) {
+690|          selected = { start: segment.index, end };
+691|          break;
+692|        }
+693|      }
+694|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + 
selected.start, head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + selected.end }];
+695|    } else {
+696|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: offset, head: offset }];
+697|      dragSelection = { start: offset, active: true };
+698|    }
+699|    finishGroup();
+700|    queueRender();
+701|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+702|  }
+703|
+704|  function handleEditorMouseMove(event) {
+705|    if (!dragSelection || !dragSelection.active) {
+706|      return;
+707|    }
+708|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+709|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+710|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+711|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+712|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+713|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+714|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: dragSelection.start, head: offset 
}];
+715|    queueRender();
+716|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+717|  }
+718|
+719|  function finishDragging() {
+720|    dragSelection = null;
+721|  }
+722|
+723|  function handleEditorShortcut(event) {
+724|    const isMac = navigator.platform.toLowerCase().includes('mac');
+725|    const mod = isMac ? event.metaKey : event.ctrlKey;
+726|    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
+727|
+728|    if (mod && key === 'f') {
+729|      event.preventDefault();
+730|      openFindBar();
+731|      return true;
+732|    }
+733|    if (mod && key === 's') {
+734|      event.preventDefault();
+735|      saveReport();
+736|      return true;
+737|    }
+738|    if (mod && key === 'z' && !event.shiftKey) {
+739|      event.preventDefault();
+740|      handleUndo();
+741|      return true;
+742|    }
+743|    if (mod && (key === 'y' || (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey))) {
+744|      event.preventDefault();
+745|      handleRedo();
+746|      return true;
+747|    }
+748|    if (mod && key === 'a') {
+749|      event.preventDefault();
+750|      selectAll();
+751|      return true;
+752|    }
+753|    return false;
+754|  }
+755|
+756|  function handleEditorKeyDown(event) {
+757|    if (handleEditorShortcut(event)) {
+758|      return;
+759|    }
+760|
+761|    const mod = event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey;
+762|    const shift = event.shiftKey;
+763|    const key = event.key;
+764|
+765|    if (key === 'Escape') {
+766|      event.preventDefault();
+767|      findInput.focus();
+768|      return;
+769|    }
+770|    if (key === 'Tab') {
+771|      event.preventDefault();
+772|      insertTab(shift);
+773|      return;
+774|    }
+775|    if (key === 'Enter') {
+776|      event.preventDefault();
+777|      typeText('\n');
+778|      return;
+779|    }
+780|    if (key === 'Backspace') {
+781|      event.preventDefault();
+782|      deleteByDirection(-1);
+783|      return;
+784|    }
+785|    if (key === 'Delete') {
+786|      event.preventDefault();
+787|      deleteByDirection(1);
+788|      return;
+789|    }
+790|    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+791|      event.preventDefault();
+792|      moveRanges(-1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+793|      return;
+794|    }
+795|    if (key === 'ArrowRight') {
+796|      event.preventDefault();
+797|      moveRanges(1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+798|      return;
+799|    }
+800|    if (key === 'ArrowUp') {
+801|      event.preventDefault();
+802|      moveVertical(-1, shift);
+803|      return;
+804|    }
+805|    if (key === 'ArrowDown') {
+806|      event.preventDefault();
+807|      moveVertical(1, shift);
+808|      return;
+809|    }
+810|    if (key === 'Home') {
+811|      event.preventDefault();
+812|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+813|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+814|        const target = mod ? 0 : info.lineStart;
+815|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+816|      });
+817|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+818|      finishGroup();
+819|      queueRender();
+820|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+821|      return;
+822|    }
+823|    if (key === 'End') {
+824|      event.preventDefault();
+825|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+826|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+827|        const target = mod ? state.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
+828|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+829|      });
+830|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+831|      finishGroup();
+832|      queueRender();
+833|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+834|      return;
+835|    }
+836|    if (mod && key === 'g') {
+837|      event.preventDefault();
+838|      navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
+839|      return;
+840|    }
+841|    if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
+842|      event.preventDefault();
+843|      typeText(key);
+844|    }
+845|  }
+846|
+847|  function handlePaste(event) {
+848|    event.preventDefault();
+849|    typeText(event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '');
+850|  }
+851|
+852|  function handleCopy(event) {
+853|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+854|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+855|      return;
+856|    }
+857|    event.preventDefault();
+858|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+859|  }
+860|
+861|  function handleCut(event) {
+862|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+863|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+864|      return;
+865|    }
+866|    event.preventDefault();
+867|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+868|    applyReplacement([range], '', 'cut');
+869|  }
+870|
+871|  function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
+872|    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+873|      event.preventDefault();
+874|      editorScroll.focus();
+875|      return;
+876|    }
+877|    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
+878|      event.preventDefault();
+879|      navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
+880|    }
+881|  }
+882|
+883|  function handleScroll() {
+884|    queueRender();
+885|  }
+886|
+887|  function handlePreviewScroll() {
+888|    previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
+889|    queueRender();
+890|  }
+891|
+892|  function handleWindowResize() {
+893|    metricsCache.clear();
+894|    queueRender();
+895|  }
+896|
+897|  function bindUi() {
+898|    editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
+899|    editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
+900|    editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
+901|    editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
+902|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
+903|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
+904|    window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
+905|    editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
+906|    previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
+907|    findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
+908|      state.findQuery = findInput.value;
+909|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+910|      findMatches();
+911|    });
+912|    findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
+913|    replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
+914|      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+915|        editorScroll.focus();
+916|      }
+917|    });
+918|    findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
+919|      findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
+920|      if (!findBar.hidden) {
+921|        findInput.focus();
+922|        findInput.select();
+923|      } else {
+924|        editorScroll.focus();
+925|      }
+926|    });
+927|    findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
+928|    findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
+929|    replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
+930|    replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
+931|    saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
+932|    reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+933|    discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+934|    undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
+935|    redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
+936|    closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
+937|    previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+938|      if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
+939|        closePreview();
+940|      }
+941|    });
+942|    window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
+943|  }
+944|
+945|  function closePreview() {
+946|    state.preview = null;
+947|    previewModal.hidden = true;
+948|    editorScroll.focus();
+949|  }
+950|
+951|  function reloadLatest() {
+952|    if (state.document) {
+953|      loadReport(state.document.id);
+954|    }
+955|  }
+956|
+957|  function handleUndo() {
+958|    if (!state.undoStack.length) {
+959|      setMessage('Nothing to undo.', 'warning');
+960|      return;
+961|    }
+962|    const current = snapshot();
+963|    const previous = state.undoStack.pop();
+964|    state.redoStack.push(current);
+965|    restoreSnapshot(previous);
+966|    setDirtyStatus();
+967|    setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
+968|  }
+969|
+970|  function handleRedo() {
+971|    if (!state.redoStack.length) {
+972|      setMessage('Nothing to redo.', 'warning');
+973|      return;
+974|    }
+975|    const current = snapshot();
+976|    const next = state.redoStack.pop();
+977|    state.undoStack.push(current);
+978|    restoreSnapshot(next);
+979|    setDirtyStatus();
+980|    setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
+981|  }
+982|
+983|  function insertTab(shiftKey) {
+984|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+985|    if (range.start !== range.end && state.content.slice(range.start, 
range.end).includes('\n')) {
+986|      indentSelection(shiftKey);
+987|      return;
+988|    }
+989|    if (shiftKey) {
+990|      indentSelection(true);
+991|      return;
+992|    }
+993|    typeText('\t');
+994|  }
+995|
+996|  function openFindBar() {
+997|    findBar.hidden = false;
+998|    findInput.focus();
+999|    findInput.select();
+1000|  }
+1001|
+1002|  function loadReport(reportId) {
+1003|    if (!reportId) {
+1004|      return Promise.resolve();
+1005|    }
+1006|    setStatus('Loading report…');
+1007|    setMessage('');
+1008|    return fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`)
+1009|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+1010|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+1011|        if (!response.ok) {
+1012|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load report.');
+1013|        }
+1014|        state.reportId = data.document.id;
+1015|        state.document = data.document;
+1016|        state.revisions = data.revisions;
+1017|        state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
+1018|        state.savedContent = data.document.content;
+1019|        state.conflict = null;
+1020|        state.undoStack = [];
+1021|        state.redoStack = [];
+1022|        state.typingGroup = null;
+1023|        state.findQuery = '';
+1024|        findInput.value = '';
+1025|        state.matches = [];
+1026|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+1027|        updateContentModel(data.document.content);
+1028|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+1029|        renderHeader();
+1030|        renderReports();
+1031|        renderHistory();
+1032|        renderFindBar();
+1033|        setDirtyStatus();
+1034|        queueRender();
+1035|        setMessage('Report loaded.', 'good');
+1036|      })
+1037|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+1038|  }
+1039|
+1040|  function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
+1041|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+1042|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+1043|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+1044|        if (!response.ok) {
+1045|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
+1046|        }
+1047|        state.preview = data;
+1048|        previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
+1049|        previewModal.hidden = false;
+1050|        previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
+1051|        previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
+1052|        renderPreview();
+1053|      })
+1054|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+1055|  }
+1056|
+1057|  function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
+1058|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+1059|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+1060|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+1061|        if (!response.ok) {
+1062|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
+1063|        }
+1064|        const before = snapshot();
+1065|        pushUndo(before, 'restore');
+1066|        updateContentModel(data.content);
+1067|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+1068|        state.matches = [];
+1069|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+1070|        renderFindBar();
+1071|        queueRender();
+1072|        setDirtyStatus();
+1073|        setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
+1074|      })
+1075|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+1076|  }
+1077|
+1078|  function renderReports() {
+1079|    Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip')).forEach((chip)
=> {
+1080|      chip.classList.toggle('active', chip.dataset.reportId === 
state.reportId);
+1081|      chip.onclick = () => loadReport(chip.dataset.reportId);
+1082|    });
+1083|  }
+1084|
+1085|  function renderHeader() {
+1086|    if (!state.document) {
+1087|      documentTitle.textContent = 'Loading…';
+1088|      documentMeta.textContent = '';
+1089|      return;
+1090|    }
+1091|    documentTitle.textContent = state.document.title;
+1092|    documentMeta.textContent = `Author: ${state.document.author} • 
${state.document.summary}`;
+1093|  }
+1094|
+1095|  function renderHistory() {
+1096|    if (!state.revisions.length) {
+1097|      historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
+1098|      return;
+1099|    }
+1100|    historyList.innerHTML = state.revisions.map((revision) => `
+1101|      <article class="history-entry">
+1102|        <div>
+1103|          <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
+1104|          <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
+1105|        </div>
+1106|        <div class="history-entry-actions">
+1107|          <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
+1108|          <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
+1109|        </div>
+1110|      </article>
+1111|    `).join('');
+1112|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+1113|      button.onclick = () => openPreview(button.dataset.previewRevision);
+1114|    });
+1115|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+1116|      button.onclick = () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision);
+1117|    });
+1118|  }
+1119|
+1120|  function renderFindBar() {
+1121|    findCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
match${state.matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
+1122|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+1123|      findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
+1124|    } else if (!state.matches.length) {
+1125|      findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
+1126|    } else {
+1127|      findStatus.textContent = `Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${state.matches.length}`;
+1128|    }
+1129|  }
+1130|
+1131|  function setDirtyStatus() {
+1132|    if (state.conflict) {
+1133|      setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${state.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
+1134|      return;
+1135|    }
+1136|    if (state.content === state.savedContent) {
+1137|      setStatus(`Saved at revision ${state.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
+1138|    } else {
+1139|      setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${state.baseRevision}`, 
'warning');
+1140|    }
+1141|  }
+1142|
+1143|  function renderStatus() {
+1144|    setDirtyStatus();
+1145|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+1146|    const column = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
+1147|    cursorState.textContent = state.selectionRanges.length > 1
+1148|      ? `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1} • 
${state.selectionRanges.length} carets`
+1149|      : `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1}`;
+1150|  }
+1151|
+1152|  function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, content, 
mode) {
+1153|    const rowHeight = state.rowHeight;
+1154|    const scrollTop = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.top : 
scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+1155|    const scrollLeft = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.left : 
scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
+1156|    const visibleHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
+1157|    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 2);
+1158|    const endLine = Math.min(content.lines.length - 1, 
Math.ceil((scrollTop + visibleHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
+1159|    const totalHeight = content.lines.length * rowHeight;
+1160|    const ranges = content.selectionRanges || [];
+1161|    const matches = content.matches || [];
+1162|    const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;
+1163|
+1164|    const rows = [];
+1165|    const gutterRows = [];
+1166|    let maxWidth = 0;
+1167|    for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 1) 
{
+1168|      const line = content.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+1169|      const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] || 0;
+1170|      const lineWidth = ensureLineMetrics(line).width;
+1171|      maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, lineWidth);
+1172|      const top = lineIndex * rowHeight;
+1173|      const selectionRects = [];
+1174|      const matchRects = [];
+1175|      const carets = [];
+1176|
+1177|      for (const range of ranges) {
+1178|        const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
+1179|        const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + line.length);
+1180|        if (start < end) {
+1181|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+1182|          selectionRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, 
end - lineStart) - left) });
+1183|        }
+1184|        if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && 
range.start <= lineStart + line.length) {
+1185|          carets.push({ left: offsetToX(line, range.start - lineStart) });
+1186|        }
+1187|      }
+1188|
+1189|      for (let index = 0; index < matches.length; index += 1) {
+1190|        const match = matches[index];
+1191|        const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
+1192|        const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + line.length);
+1193|        if (start < end) {
+1194|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+1195|          matchRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end -
lineStart) - left), current: index === activeMatchIndex });
+1196|        }
+1197|      }
+1198|
+1199|      rows.push(`
+1200|        <div class="line-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;">
+1201|          ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div class="match-rect${rect.current
? ' current-match' : ''}" style="left:${rect.left}px; 
width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+1202|          ${selectionRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+1203|          ${carets.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+1204|          <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>
+1205|        </div>
+1206|      `);
+1207|      gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
+1208|    }
+1209|
+1210|    scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px; min-width:${maxWidth + 
200}px;">${rows.join('')}</div>`;
+1211|    gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
+1212|    scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
+1213|    scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
+1214|  }
+1215|
+1216|  function renderEditor() {
+1217|    if (!state.document) {
+1218|      return;
+1219|    }
+1220|    renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
+1221|      lines: state.lines,
+1222|      lineStarts: state.lineStarts,
+1223|      selectionRanges: state.selectionRanges,
+1224|      matches: state.matches,
+1225|      activeMatchIndex: state.activeMatchIndex,
+1226|    }, 'editor');
+1227|    renderStatus();
+1228|  }
+1229|
+1230|  function renderPreview() {
+1231|    if (!state.preview) {
+1232|      return;
+1233|    }
+1234|    const lines = state.preview.content.split('\n');
+1235|    const lineStarts = [0];
+1236|    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
+1237|      lineStarts[index + 1] = lineStarts[index] + lines[index].length + 
(index < lines.length - 1 ? 1 : 0);
+1238|    }
+1239|    renderEditorSurface(previewScroll, previewGutter, {
+1240|      lines,
+1241|      lineStarts,
+1242|      selectionRanges: [],
+1243|      matches: [],
+1244|      activeMatchIndex: -1,
+1245|    }, 'preview');
+1246|  }
+1247|
+1248|  function queueRender() {
+1249|    if (renderQueued) {
+1250|      return;
+1251|    }
+1252|    renderQueued = true;
+1253|    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+1254|      renderQueued = false;
+1255|      renderEditor();
+1256|      if (state.preview) {
+1257|        renderPreview();
+1258|      }
+1259|    });
+1260|  }
+1261|
+1262|  function scrollSelectionIntoView() {
+1263|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+1264|    const caretX = offsetToX(info.line, info.columnOffset);
+1265|    const caretY = info.lineIndex * state.rowHeight;
+1266|    if (caretY < editorScroll.scrollTop + 16) {
+1267|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - state.rowHeight * 3);
+1268|    } else if (caretY + state.rowHeight > editorScroll.scrollTop + 
editorScroll.clientHeight - 16) {
+1269|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - 
editorScroll.clientHeight + state.rowHeight * 4);
+1270|    }
+1271|    if (caretX < editorScroll.scrollLeft + 24) {
+1272|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 40);
+1273|    } else if (caretX > editorScroll.scrollLeft + editorScroll.clientWidth
- 24) {
+1274|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 
editorScroll.clientWidth + 80);
+1275|    }
+1276|  }
+1277|
+1278|  function handleEditorMouseDown(event) {
+1279|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+1280|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+1281|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+1282|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+1283|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+1284|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+1285|
+1286|    if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
+1287|      state.selectionRanges = [...state.selectionRanges, { anchor: offset,
head: offset }];
+1288|      finishGroup();
+1289|      queueRender();
+1290|      return;
+1291|    }
+1292|
+1293|    if (event.detail >= 3) {
+1294|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex], 
head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + line.length }];
+1295|    } else if (event.detail === 2 && segmenterWord) {
+1296|      const relative = offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex];
+1297|      const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
+1298|      let selected = { start: 0, end: line.length };
+1299|      for (const segment of segments) {
+1300|        const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
+1301|        if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index <= relative && relative <=
end) {
+1302|          selected = { start: segment.index, end };
+1303|          break;
+1304|        }
+1305|      }
+1306|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + 
selected.start, head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + selected.end }];
+1307|    } else {
+1308|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: offset, head: offset }];
+1309|      dragSelection = { start: offset, active: true };
+1310|    }
+1311|    finishGroup();
+1312|    queueRender();
+1313|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+1314|  }
+1315|
+1316|  function handleEditorMouseMove(event) {
+1317|    if (!dragSelection || !dragSelection.active) {
+1318|      return;
+1319|    }
+1320|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+1321|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+1322|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+1323|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+1324|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+1325|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+1326|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: dragSelection.start, head: offset 
}];
+1327|    queueRender();
+1328|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+1329|  }
+1330|
+1331|  function finishDragging() {
+1332|    dragSelection = null;
+1333|  }
+1334|
+1335|  function handleEditorShortcut(event) {
+1336|    const isMac = navigator.platform.toLowerCase().includes('mac');
+1337|    const mod = isMac ? event.metaKey : event.ctrlKey;
+1338|    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
+1339|
+1340|    if (mod && key === 'f') {
+1341|      event.preventDefault();
+1342|      openFindBar();
+1343|      return true;
+1344|    }
+1345|    if (mod && key === 's') {
+1346|      event.preventDefault();
+1347|      saveReport();
+1348|      return true;
+1349|    }
+1350|    if (mod && key === 'z' && !event.shiftKey) {
+1351|      event.preventDefault();
+1352|      handleUndo();
+1353|      return true;
+1354|    }
+1355|    if (mod && (key === 'y' || (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey))) {
+1356|      event.preventDefault();
+1357|      handleRedo();
+1358|      return true;
+1359|    }
+1360|    if (mod && key === 'a') {
+1361|      event.preventDefault();
+1362|      selectAll();
+1363|      return true;
+1364|    }
+1365|    return false;
+1366|  }
+1367|
+1368|  function handleEditorKeyDown(event) {
+1369|    if (handleEditorShortcut(event)) {
+1370|      return;
+1371|    }
+1372|
+1373|    const mod = event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey;
+1374|    const shift = event.shiftKey;
+1375|    const key = event.key;
+1376|
+1377|    if (key === 'Escape') {
+1378|      event.preventDefault();
+1379|      findInput.focus();
+1380|      return;
+1381|    }
+1382|    if (key === 'Tab') {
+1383|      event.preventDefault();
+1384|      insertTab(shift);
+1385|      return;
+1386|    }
+1387|    if (key === 'Enter') {
+1388|      event.preventDefault();
+1389|      typeText('\n');
+1390|      return;
+1391|    }
+1392|    if (key === 'Backspace') {
+1393|      event.preventDefault();
+1394|      deleteByDirection(-1);
+1395|      return;
+1396|    }
+1397|    if (key === 'Delete') {
+1398|      event.preventDefault();
+1399|      deleteByDirection(1);
+1400|      return;
+1401|    }
+1402|    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+1403|      event.preventDefault();
+1404|      moveRanges(-1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+1405|      return;
+1406|    }
+1407|    if (key === 'ArrowRight') {
+1408|      event.preventDefault();
+1409|      moveRanges(1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+1410|      return;
+1411|    }
+1412|    if (key === 'ArrowUp') {
+1413|      event.preventDefault();
+1414|      moveVertical(-1, shift);
+1415|      return;
+1416|    }
+1417|    if (key === 'ArrowDown') {
+1418|      event.preventDefault();
+1419|      moveVertical(1, shift);
+1420|      return;
+1421|    }
+1422|    if (key === 'Home') {
+1423|      event.preventDefault();
+1424|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+1425|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+1426|        const target = mod ? 0 : info.lineStart;
+1427|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+1428|      });
+1429|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+1430|      finishGroup();
+1431|      queueRender();
+1432|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+1433|      return;
+1434|    }
+1435|    if (key === 'End') {
+1436|      event.preventDefault();
+1437|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+1438|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+1439|        const target = mod ? state.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
+1440|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+1441|      });
+1442|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+1443|      finishGroup();
+1444|      queueRender();
+1445|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+1446|      return;
+1447|    }
+1448|    if (mod && key === 'g') {
+1449|      event.preventDefault();
+1450|      navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
+1451|      return;
+1452|    }
+1453|    if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
+1454|      event.preventDefault();
+1455|      typeText(key);
+1456|    }
+1457|  }
+1458|
+1459|  function handlePaste(event) {
+1460|    event.preventDefault();
+1461|    typeText(event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '');
+1462|  }
+1463|
+1464|  function handleCopy(event) {
+1465|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+1466|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+1467|      return;
+1468|    }
+1469|    event.preventDefault();
+1470|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+1471|  }
+1472|
+1473|  function handleCut(event) {
+1474|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+1475|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+1476|      return;
+1477|    }
+1478|    event.preventDefault();
+1479|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+1480|    applyReplacement([range], '', 'cut');
+1481|  }
+1482|
+1483|  function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
+1484|    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+1485|      event.preventDefault();
+1486|      editorScroll.focus();
+1487|      return;
+1488|    }
+1489|    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
+1490|      event.preventDefault();
+1491|      navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
+1492|    }
+1493|  }
+1494|
+1495|  function handleScroll() {
+1496|    queueRender();
+1497|  }
+1498|
+1499|  function handlePreviewScroll() {
+1500|    previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
+1501|    queueRender();
+1502|  }
+1503|
+1504|  function handleWindowResize() {
+1505|    metricsCache.clear();
+1506|    queueRender();
+1507|  }
+1508|
+1509|  function bindUi() {
+1510|    editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
+1511|    editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
+1512|    editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
+1513|    editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
+1514|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
+1515|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
+1516|    window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
+1517|    editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
+1518|    previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
+1519|    findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
+1520|      state.findQuery = findInput.value;
+1521|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+1522|      findMatches();
+1523|    });
+1524|    findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
+1525|    replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
+1526|      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+1527|        editorScroll.focus();
+1528|      }
+1529|    });
+1530|    findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
+1531|      findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
+1532|      if (!findBar.hidden) {
+1533|        findInput.focus();
+1534|        findInput.select();
+1535|      } else {
+1536|        editorScroll.focus();
+1537|      }
+1538|    });
+1539|    findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
+1540|    findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
+1541|    replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
+1542|    replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
+1543|    saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
+1544|    reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+1545|    discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+1546|    undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
+1547|    redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
+1548|    closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
+1549|    previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+1550|      if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
+1551|        closePreview();
+1552|      }
+1553|    });
+1554|    window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
+1555|  }
+1556|
+1557|  function closePreview() {
+1558|    state.preview = null;
+1559|    previewModal.hidden = true;
+1560|    editorScroll.focus();
+1561|  }
+1562|
+1563|  function reloadLatest() {
+1564|    if (state.document) {
+1565|      loadReport(state.document.id);
+1566|    }
+1567|  }
+1568|
+1569|  function handleUndo() {
+1570|    if (!state.undoStack.length) {
+1571|      setMessage('Nothing to undo.', 'warning');
+1572|      return;
+1573|    }
+1574|    const current = snapshot();
+1575|    const previous = state.undoStack.pop();
+1576|    state.redoStack.push(current);
+1577|    restoreSnapshot(previous);
+1578|    setDirtyStatus();
+1579|    setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
+1580|  }
+1581|
+1582|  function handleRedo() {
+1583|    if (!state.redoStack.length) {
+1584|      setMessage('Nothing to redo.', 'warning');
+1585|      return;
+1586|    }
+1587|    const current = snapshot();
+1588|    const next = state.redoStack.pop();
+1589|    state.undoStack.push(current);
+1590|    restoreSnapshot(next);
+1591|    setDirtyStatus();
+1592|    setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
+1593|  }
+1594|
+1595|  function insertTab(shiftKey) {
+1596|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+1597|    if (range.start !== range.end && state.content.slice(range.start, 
range.end).includes('\n')) {
+1598|      indentSelection(shiftKey);
+1599|      return;
+1600|    }
+1601|    if (shiftKey) {
+1602|      indentSelection(true);
+1603|      return;
+1604|    }
+1605|    typeText('\t');
+1606|  }
+1607|
+1608|  function openFindBar() {
+1609|    findBar.hidden = false;
+1610|    findInput.focus();
+1611|    findInput.select();
+1612|  }
+1613|
+1614|  function loadReport(reportId) {
+1615|    if (!reportId) {
+1616|      return Promise.resolve();
+1617|    }
+1618|    setStatus('Loading report…');
+1619|    setMessage('');
+1620|    return fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`)
+1621|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+1622|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+1623|        if (!response.ok) {
+1624|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load report.');
+1625|        }
+1626|        state.reportId = data.document.id;
+1627|        state.document = data.document;
+1628|        state.revisions = data.revisions;
+1629|        state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
+1630|        state.savedContent = data.document.content;
+1631|        state.conflict = null;
+1632|        state.undoStack = [];
+1633|        state.redoStack = [];
+1634|        state.typingGroup = null;
+1635|        state.findQuery = '';
+1636|        findInput.value = '';
+1637|        state.matches = [];
+1638|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+1639|        updateContentModel(data.document.content);
+1640|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+1641|        renderHeader();
+1642|        renderReports();
+1643|        renderHistory();
+1644|        renderFindBar();
+1645|        setDirtyStatus();
+1646|        queueRender();
+1647|        setMessage('Report loaded.', 'good');
+1648|      })
+1649|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+1650|  }
+1651|
+1652|  function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
+1653|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+1654|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+1655|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+1656|        if (!response.ok) {
+1657|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
+1658|        }
+1659|        state.preview = data;
+1660|        previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
+1661|        previewModal.hidden = false;
+1662|        previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
+1663|        previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
+1664|        renderPreview();
+1665|      })
+1666|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+1667|  }
+1668|
+1669|  function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
+1670|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+1671|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+1672|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+1673|        if (!response.ok) {
+1674|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
+1675|        }
+1676|        const before = snapshot();
+1677|        pushUndo(before, 'restore');
+1678|        updateContentModel(data.content);
+1679|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+1680|        state.matches = [];
+1681|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+1682|        renderFindBar();
+1683|        queueRender();
+1684|        setDirtyStatus();
+1685|        setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
+1686|      })
+1687|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+1688|  }
+1689|
+1690|  function renderReports() {
+1691|    Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip')).forEach((chip)
=> {
+1692|      chip.classList.toggle('active', chip.dataset.reportId === 
state.reportId);
+1693|      chip.onclick = () => loadReport(chip.dataset.reportId);
+1694|    });
+1695|  }
+1696|
+1697|  function renderHeader() {
+1698|    if (!state.document) {
+1699|      documentTitle.textContent = 'Loading…';
+1700|      documentMeta.textContent = '';
+1701|      return;
+1702|    }
+1703|    documentTitle.textContent = state.document.title;
+1704|    documentMeta.textContent = `Author: ${state.document.author} • 
${state.document.summary}`;
+1705|  }
+1706|
+1707|  function renderHistory() {
+1708|    if (!state.revisions.length) {
+1709|      historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
+1710|      return;
+1711|    }
+1712|    historyList.innerHTML = state.revisions.map((revision) => `
+1713|      <article class="history-entry">
+1714|        <div>
+1715|          <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
+1716|          <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
+1717|        </div>
+1718|        <div class="history-entry-actions">
+1719|          <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
+1720|          <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
+1721|        </div>
+1722|      </article>
+1723|    `).join('');
+1724|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+1725|      button.onclick = () => openPreview(button.dataset.previewRevision);
+1726|    });
+1727|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+1728|      button.onclick = () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision);
+1729|    });
+1730|  }
+1731|
+1732|  function renderFindBar() {
+1733|    findCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
match${state.matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
+1734|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+1735|      findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
+1736|    } else if (!state.matches.length) {
+1737|      findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
+1738|    } else {
+1739|      findStatus.textContent = `Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${state.matches.length}`;
+1740|    }
+1741|  }
+1742|
+1743|  function setDirtyStatus() {
+1744|    if (state.conflict) {
+1745|      setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${state.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
+1746|      return;
+1747|    }
+1748|    if (state.content === state.savedContent) {
+1749|      setStatus(`Saved at revision ${state.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
+1750|    } else {
+1751|      setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${state.baseRevision}`, 
'warning');
+1752|    }
+1753|  }
+1754|
+1755|  function renderStatus() {
+1756|    setDirtyStatus();
+1757|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+1758|    const column = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
+1759|    cursorState.textContent = state.selectionRanges.length > 1
+1760|      ? `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1} • 
${state.selectionRanges.length} carets`
+1761|      : `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1}`;
+1762|  }
+1763|
+1764|  function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, content, 
mode) {
+1765|    const rowHeight = state.rowHeight;
+1766|    const scrollTop = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.top : 
scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+1767|    const scrollLeft = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.left : 
scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
+1768|    const visibleHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
+1769|    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 2);
+1770|    const endLine = Math.min(content.lines.length - 1, 
Math.ceil((scrollTop + visibleHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
+1771|    const totalHeight = content.lines.length * rowHeight;
+1772|    const ranges = content.selectionRanges || [];
+1773|    const matches = content.matches || [];
+1774|    const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;
+1775|
+1776|    const rows = [];
+1777|    const gutterRows = [];
+1778|    let maxWidth = 0;
+1779|    for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 1) 
{
+1780|      const line = content.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+1781|      const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] || 0;
+1782|      const lineWidth = ensureLineMetrics(line).width;
+1783|      maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, lineWidth);
+1784|      const top = lineIndex * rowHeight;
+1785|      const selectionRects = [];
+1786|      const matchRects = [];
+1787|      const carets = [];
+1788|
+1789|      for (const range of ranges) {
+1790|        const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
+1791|        const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + line.length);
+1792|        if (start < end) {
+1793|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+1794|          selectionRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, 
end - lineStart) - left) });
+1795|        }
+1796|        if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && 
range.start <= lineStart + line.length) {
+1797|          carets.push({ left: offsetToX(line, range.start - lineStart) });
+1798|        }
+1799|      }
+1800|
+1801|      for (let index = 0; index < matches.length; index += 1) {
+1802|        const match = matches[index];
+1803|        const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
+1804|        const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + line.length);
+1805|        if (start < end) {
+1806|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+1807|          matchRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end -
lineStart) - left), current: index === activeMatchIndex });
+1808|        }
+1809|      }
+1810|
+1811|      rows.push(`
+1812|        <div class="line-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;">
+1813|          ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div class="match-rect${rect.current
? ' current-match' : ''}" style="left:${rect.left}px; 
width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+1814|          ${selectionRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+1815|          ${carets.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+1816|          <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>
+1817|        </div>
+1818|      `);
+1819|      gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
+1820|    }
+1821|
+1822|    scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px; min-width:${maxWidth + 
200}px;">${rows.join('')}</div>`;
+1823|    gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
+1824|    scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
+1825|    scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
+1826|  }
+1827|
+1828|  function renderEditor() {
+1829|    if (!state.document) {
+1830|      return;
+1831|    }
+1832|    renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
+1833|      lines: state.lines,
+1834|      lineStarts: state.lineStarts,
+1835|      selectionRanges: state.selectionRanges,
+1836|      matches: state.matches,
+1837|      activeMatchIndex: state.activeMatchIndex,
+1838|    }, 'editor');
+1839|    renderStatus();
+1840|  }
+1841|
+1842|  function renderPreview() {
+1843|    if (!state.preview) {
+1844|      return;
+1845|    }
+1846|    const lines = state.preview.content.split('\n');
+1847|    const lineStarts = [0];
+1848|    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
+1849|      lineStarts[index + 1] = lineStarts[index] + lines[index].length + 
(index < lines.length - 1 ? 1 : 0);
+1850|    }
+1851|    renderEditorSurface(previewScroll, previewGutter, {
+1852|      lines,
+1853|      lineStarts,
+1854|      selectionRanges: [],
+1855|      matches: [],
+1856|      activeMatchIndex: -1,
+1857|    }, 'preview');
+1858|  }
+1859|
+1860|  function queueRender() {
+1861|    if (renderQueued) {
+1862|      return;
+1863|    }
+1864|    renderQueued = true;
+1865|    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+1866|      renderQueued = false;
+1867|      renderEditor();
+1868|      if (state.preview) {
+1869|        renderPreview();
+1870|      }
+1871|    });
+1872|  }
+1873|
+1874|  function scrollSelectionIntoView() {
+1875|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+1876|    const caretX = offsetToX(info.line, info.columnOffset);
+1877|    const caretY = info.lineIndex * state.rowHeight;
+1878|    if (caretY < editorScroll.scrollTop + 16) {
+1879|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - state.rowHeight * 3);
+1880|    } else if (caretY + state.rowHeight > editorScroll.scrollTop + 
editorScroll.clientHeight - 16) {
+1881|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - 
editorScroll.clientHeight + state.rowHeight * 4);
+1882|    }
+1883|    if (caretX < editorScroll.scrollLeft + 24) {
+1884|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 40);
+1885|    } else if (caretX > editorScroll.scrollLeft + editorScroll.clientWidth
- 24) {
+1886|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 
editorScroll.clientWidth + 80);
+1887|    }
+1888|  }
+1889|
+1890|  function handleEditorMouseDown(event) {
+1891|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+1892|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+1893|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+1894|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+1895|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+1896|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+1897|
+1898|    if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
+1899|      state.selectionRanges = [...state.selectionRanges, { anchor: offset,
head: offset }];
+1900|      finishGroup();
+1901|      queueRender();
+1902|      return;
+1903|    }
+1904|
+1905|    if (event.detail >= 3) {
+1906|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex], 
head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + line.length }];
+1907|    } else if (event.detail === 2 && segmenterWord) {
+1908|      const relative = offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex];
+1909|      const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
+1910|      let selected = { start: 0, end: line.length };
+1911|      for (const segment of segments) {
+1912|        const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
+1913|        if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index <= relative && relative <=
end) {
+1914|          selected = { start: segment.index, end };
+1915|          break;
+1916|        }
+1917|      }
+1918|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + 
selected.start, head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + selected.end }];
+1919|    } else {
+1920|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: offset, head: offset }];
+1921|      dragSelection = { start: offset, active: true };
+1922|    }
+1923|    finishGroup();
+1924|    queueRender();
+1925|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+1926|  }
+1927|
+1928|  function handleEditorMouseMove(event) {
+1929|    if (!dragSelection || !dragSelection.active) {
+1930|      return;
+1931|    }
+1932|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+1933|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+1934|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+1935|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+1936|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+1937|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+1938|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: dragSelection.start, head: offset 
}];
+1939|    queueRender();
+1940|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+1941|  }
+1942|
+1943|  function finishDragging() {
+1944|    dragSelection = null;
+1945|  }
+1946|
+1947|  function handleEditorShortcut(event) {
+1948|    const isMac = navigator.platform.toLowerCase().includes('mac');
+1949|    const mod = isMac ? event.metaKey : event.ctrlKey;
+1950|    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
+1951|
+1952|    if (mod && key === 'f') {
+1953|      event.preventDefault();
+1954|      openFindBar();
+1955|      return true;
+1956|    }
+1957|    if (mod && key === 's') {
+1958|      event.preventDefault();
+1959|      saveReport();
+1960|      return true;
+1961|    }
+1962|    if (mod && key === 'z' && !event.shiftKey) {
+1963|      event.preventDefault();
+1964|      handleUndo();
+1965|      return true;
+1966|    }
+1967|    if (mod && (key === 'y' || (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey))) {
+1968|      event.preventDefault();
+1969|      handleRedo();
+1970|      return true;
+1971|    }
+1972|    if (mod && key === 'a') {
+1973|      event.preventDefault();
+1974|      selectAll();
+1975|      return true;
+1976|    }
+1977|    return false;
+1978|  }
+1979|
+1980|  function handleEditorKeyDown(event) {
+1981|    if (handleEditorShortcut(event)) {
+1982|      return;
+1983|    }
+1984|
+1985|    const mod = event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey;
+1986|    const shift = event.shiftKey;
+1987|    const key = event.key;
+1988|
+1989|    if (key === 'Escape') {
+1990|      event.preventDefault();
+1991|      findInput.focus();
+1992|      return;
+1993|    }
+1994|    if (key === 'Tab') {
+1995|      event.preventDefault();
+1996|      insertTab(shift);
+1997|      return;
+1998|    }
+1999|    if (key === 'Enter') {
+2000|      event.preventDefault();
+2001|      typeText('\n');
+2002|      return;
+2003|    }
+2004|    if (key === 'Backspace') {
+2005|      event.preventDefault();
+2006|      deleteByDirection(-1);
+2007|      return;
+2008|    }
+2009|    if (key === 'Delete') {
+2010|      event.preventDefault();
+2011|      deleteByDirection(1);
+2012|      return;
+2013|    }
+2014|    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+2015|      event.preventDefault();
+2016|      moveRanges(-1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+2017|      return;
+2018|    }
+2019|    if (key === 'ArrowRight') {
+2020|      event.preventDefault();
+2021|      moveRanges(1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+2022|      return;
+2023|    }
+2024|    if (key === 'ArrowUp') {
+2025|      event.preventDefault();
+2026|      moveVertical(-1, shift);
+2027|      return;
+2028|    }
+2029|    if (key === 'ArrowDown') {
+2030|      event.preventDefault();
+2031|      moveVertical(1, shift);
+2032|      return;
+2033|    }
+2034|    if (key === 'Home') {
+2035|      event.preventDefault();
+2036|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+2037|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+2038|        const target = mod ? 0 : info.lineStart;
+2039|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+2040|      });
+2041|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+2042|      finishGroup();
+2043|      queueRender();
+2044|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+2045|      return;
+2046|    }
+2047|    if (key === 'End') {
+2048|      event.preventDefault();
+2049|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+2050|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+2051|        const target = mod ? state.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
+2052|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+2053|      });
+2054|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+2055|      finishGroup();
+2056|      queueRender();
+2057|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+2058|      return;
+2059|    }
+2060|    if (mod && key === 'g') {
+2061|      event.preventDefault();
+2062|      navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
+2063|      return;
+2064|    }
+2065|    if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
+2066|      event.preventDefault();
+2067|      typeText(key);
+2068|    }
+2069|  }
+2070|
+2071|  function handlePaste(event) {
+2072|    event.preventDefault();
+2073|    typeText(event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '');
+2074|  }
+2075|
+2076|  function handleCopy(event) {
+2077|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+2078|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+2079|      return;
+2080|    }
+2081|    event.preventDefault();
+2082|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+2083|  }
+2084|
+2085|  function handleCut(event) {
+2086|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+2087|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+2088|      return;
+2089|    }
+2090|    event.preventDefault();
+2091|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+2092|    applyReplacement([range], '', 'cut');
+2093|  }
+2094|
+2095|  function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
+2096|    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+2097|      event.preventDefault();
+2098|      editorScroll.focus();
+2099|      return;
+2100|    }
+2101|    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
+2102|      event.preventDefault();
+2103|      navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
+2104|    }
+2105|  }
+2106|
+2107|  function handleScroll() {
+2108|    queueRender();
+2109|  }
+2110|
+2111|  function handlePreviewScroll() {
+2112|    previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
+2113|    queueRender();
+2114|  }
+2115|
+2116|  function handleWindowResize() {
+2117|    metricsCache.clear();
+2118|    queueRender();
+2119|  }

+2121|  function bindUi() {
+2122|    editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
+2123|    editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
+2124|    editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
+2125|    editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
+2126|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
+2127|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
+2128|    window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
+2129|    editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
+2130|    previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
+2131|    findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
+2132|      state.findQuery = findInput.value;
+2133|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+2134|      findMatches();
+2135|    });
+2136|    findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
+2137|    replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
+2138|      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+2139|        editorScroll.focus();
+2140|      }
+2141|    });
+2142|    findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
+2143|      findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
+2144|      if (!findBar.hidden) {
+2145|        findInput.focus();
+2146|        findInput.select();
+2147|      } else {
+2148|        editorScroll.focus();
+2149|      }
+2150|    });
+2151|    findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
+2152|    findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
+2153|    replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
+2154|    replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
+2155|    saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
+2156|    reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+2157|    discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+2158|    undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
+2159|    redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
+2160|    closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
+2161|    previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+2162|      if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
+2163|        closePreview();
+2164|      }
+2165|    });
+2166|    window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
+2167|  }

+2169|  function closePreview() {
+2170|    state.preview = null;
+2171|    previewModal.hidden = true;
+2172|    editorScroll.focus();

+2175|  function reloadLatest() {
+2176|    if (state.document) {
+2177|      loadReport(state.document.id);
+2178|    }
+2179|  }
+2180|
+2181|  function handleUndo() {
+2182|    if (!state.undoStack.length) {
+2183|      setMessage('Nothing to undo.', 'warning');
+2184|      return;
+2185|    }
+2186|    const current = snapshot();
+2187|    const previous = state.undoStack.pop();
+2188|    state.redoStack.push(current);
+2189|    restoreSnapshot(previous);
+2190|    setDirtyStatus();
+2191|    setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
+2192|  }
+2193|
+2194|  function handleRedo() {
+2195|    if (!state.redoStack.length) {
+2196|      setMessage('Nothing to redo.', 'warning');
+2197|      return;
+2198|    }
+2199|    const current = snapshot();
+2200|    const next = state.redoStack.pop();
+2201|    state.undoStack.push(current);
+2202|    restoreSnapshot(next);
+2203|    setDirtyStatus();
+2204|    setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
+2205|  }
+2206|
+2207|  function insertTab(shiftKey) {
+2208|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+2209|    if (range.start !== range.end && state.content.slice(range.start, 
range.end).includes('\n')) {
+2210|      indentSelection(shiftKey);
+2211|      return;
+2212|    }
+2213|    if (shiftKey) {
+2214|      indentSelection(true);
+2215|      return;
+2216|    }
+2217|    typeText('\t');
+2218|  }
+2219|
+2220|  function openFindBar() {
+2221|    findBar.hidden = false;
+2222|    findInput.focus();
+2223|    findInput.select();
+2224|  }
+2225|
+2226|  function loadReport(reportId) {
+2227|    if (!reportId) {
+2228|      return Promise.resolve();
+2229|    }
+2230|    setStatus('Loading report…');
+2231|    setMessage('');
+2232|    return fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`)
+2233|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+2234|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+2235|        if (!response.ok) {
+2236|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to load report.');
+2237|        }
+2238|        state.reportId = data.document.id;
+2239|        state.document = data.document;
+2240|        state.revisions = data.revisions;
+2241|        state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
+2242|        state.savedContent = data.document.content;
+2243|        state.conflict = null;
+2244|        state.undoStack = [];
+2245|        state.redoStack = [];
+2246|        state.typingGroup = null;
+2247|        state.findQuery = '';
+2248|        findInput.value = '';
+2249|        state.matches = [];
+2250|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+2251|        updateContentModel(data.document.content);
+2252|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+2253|        renderHeader();
+2254|        renderReports();
+2255|        renderHistory();
+2256|        renderFindBar();
+2257|        setDirtyStatus();
+2258|        queueRender();
+2259|        setMessage('Report loaded.', 'good');
+2260|      })
+2261|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+2262|  }
+2263|
+2264|  function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
+2265|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+2266|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+2267|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+2268|        if (!response.ok) {
+2269|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
+2270|        }
+2271|        state.preview = data;
+2272|        previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
+2273|        previewModal.hidden = false;
+2274|        previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
+2275|        previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
+2276|        renderPreview();
+2277|      })
+2278|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+2279|  }
+2280|
+2281|  function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
+2282|    
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
+2283|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))
+2284|      .then(({ response, data }) => {
+2285|        if (!response.ok) {
+2286|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
+2287|        }
+2288|        const before = snapshot();
+2289|        pushUndo(before, 'restore');
+2290|        updateContentModel(data.content);
+2291|        state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+2292|        state.matches = [];
+2293|        state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+2294|        renderFindBar();
+2295|        queueRender();
+2296|        setDirtyStatus();
+2297|        setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
+2298|      })
+2299|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+2300|  }
+2301|
+2302|  function renderReports() {
+2303|    Array.from(reportList.querySelectorAll('.report-chip')).forEach((chip)
=> {
+2304|      chip.classList.toggle('active', chip.dataset.reportId === 
state.reportId);
+2305|      chip.onclick = () => loadReport(chip.dataset.reportId);
+2306|    });
+2307|  }

+2309|  function renderHeader() {
