+2312|      documentMeta.textContent = '';
+2313|      return;
+2314|    }
+2315|    documentTitle.textContent = state.document.title;
+2316|    documentMeta.textContent = `Author: ${state.document.author} • 
${state.document.summary}`;
+2317|  }
+2318|
+2319|  function renderHistory() {
+2320|    if (!state.revisions.length) {
+2321|      historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
+2322|      return;
+2323|    }
+2324|    historyList.innerHTML = state.revisions.map((revision) => `
+2325|      <article class="history-entry">
+2326|        <div>
+2327|          <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
+2328|          <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
+2329|        </div>
+2330|        <div class="history-entry-actions">
+2331|          <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
+2332|          <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
+2333|        </div>
+2334|      </article>
+2335|    `).join('');
+2336|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+2337|      button.onclick = () => openPreview(button.dataset.previewRevision);
+2338|    });
+2339|    
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]').forEach((button) => {
+2340|      button.onclick = () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision);
+2341|    });
+2342|  }
+2343|
+2344|  function renderFindBar() {
+2345|    findCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
match${state.matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
+2346|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+2347|      findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
+2348|    } else if (!state.matches.length) {
+2349|      findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
+2350|    } else {
+2351|      findStatus.textContent = `Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${state.matches.length}`;
+2352|    }
+2353|  }
+2354|
+2355|  function setDirtyStatus() {
+2356|    if (state.conflict) {
+2357|      setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${state.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
+2358|      return;
+2359|    }
+2360|    if (state.content === state.savedContent) {
+2361|      setStatus(`Saved at revision ${state.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
+2362|    } else {
+2363|      setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${state.baseRevision}`, 
'warning');
+2364|    }
+2365|  }
+2366|
+2367|  function renderStatus() {
+2368|    setDirtyStatus();
+2369|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+2370|    const column = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
+2371|    cursorState.textContent = state.selectionRanges.length > 1
+2372|      ? `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1} • 
${state.selectionRanges.length} carets`
+2373|      : `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1}`;
+2374|  }
+2375|
+2376|  function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, content, 
mode) {
+2377|    const rowHeight = state.rowHeight;
+2378|    const scrollTop = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.top : 
scrollContainer.scrollTop;
+2379|    const scrollLeft = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.left : 
scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
+2380|    const visibleHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
+2381|    const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 2);
+2382|    const endLine = Math.min(content.lines.length - 1, 
Math.ceil((scrollTop + visibleHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
+2383|    const totalHeight = content.lines.length * rowHeight;
+2384|    const ranges = content.selectionRanges || [];
+2385|    const matches = content.matches || [];
+2386|    const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;
+2387|
+2388|    const rows = [];
+2389|    const gutterRows = [];
+2390|    let maxWidth = 0;
+2391|    for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 1) 
{
+2392|      const line = content.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+2393|      const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] || 0;
+2394|      const lineWidth = ensureLineMetrics(line).width;
+2395|      maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, lineWidth);
+2396|      const top = lineIndex * rowHeight;
+2397|      const selectionRects = [];
+2398|      const matchRects = [];
+2399|      const carets = [];
+2400|
+2401|      for (const range of ranges) {
+2402|        const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
+2403|        const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + line.length);
+2404|        if (start < end) {
+2405|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+2406|          selectionRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, 
end - lineStart) - left) });
+2407|        }
+2408|        if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && 
range.start <= lineStart + line.length) {
+2409|          carets.push({ left: offsetToX(line, range.start - lineStart) });
+2410|        }
+2411|      }
+2412|
+2413|      for (let index = 0; index < matches.length; index += 1) {
+2414|        const match = matches[index];
+2415|        const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
+2416|        const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + line.length);
+2417|        if (start < end) {
+2418|          const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
+2419|          matchRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end -
lineStart) - left), current: index === activeMatchIndex });
+2420|        }
+2421|      }
+2422|
+2423|      rows.push(`
+2424|        <div class="line-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;">
+2425|          ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div class="match-rect${rect.current
? ' current-match' : ''}" style="left:${rect.left}px; 
width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+2426|          ${selectionRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+2427|          ${carets.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
+2428|          <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>
+2429|        </div>
+2430|      `);
+2431|      gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
+2432|    }
+2433|
+2434|    scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px; min-width:${maxWidth + 
200}px;">${rows.join('')}</div>`;
+2435|    gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
+2436|    scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
+2437|    scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
+2438|  }
+2439|
+2440|  function renderEditor() {
+2441|    if (!state.document) {
+2442|      return;
+2443|    }
+2444|    renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
+2445|      lines: state.lines,
+2446|      lineStarts: state.lineStarts,
+2447|      selectionRanges: state.selectionRanges,
+2448|      matches: state.matches,
+2449|      activeMatchIndex: state.activeMatchIndex,
+2450|    }, 'editor');
+2451|    renderStatus();
+2452|  }
+2453|
+2454|  function renderPreview() {
+2455|    if (!state.preview) {
+2456|      return;
+2457|    }
+2458|    const lines = state.preview.content.split('\n');
+2459|    const lineStarts = [0];
+2460|    for (let index = 0; index < lines.length; index += 1) {
+2461|      lineStarts[index + 1] = lineStarts[index] + lines[index].length + 
(index < lines.length - 1 ? 1 : 0);
+2462|    }
+2463|    renderEditorSurface(previewScroll, previewGutter, {
+2464|      lines,
+2465|      lineStarts,
+2466|      selectionRanges: [],
+2467|      matches: [],
+2468|      activeMatchIndex: -1,
+2469|    }, 'preview');
+2470|  }
+2471|
+2472|  function queueRender() {
+2473|    if (renderQueued) {
+2474|      return;
+2475|    }
+2476|    renderQueued = true;
+2477|    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+2478|      renderQueued = false;
+2479|      renderEditor();
+2480|      if (state.preview) {
+2481|        renderPreview();
+2482|      }
+2483|    });
+2484|  }
+2485|
+2486|  function scrollSelectionIntoView() {
+2487|    const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
+2488|    const caretX = offsetToX(info.line, info.columnOffset);
+2489|    const caretY = info.lineIndex * state.rowHeight;
+2490|    if (caretY < editorScroll.scrollTop + 16) {
+2491|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - state.rowHeight * 3);
+2492|    } else if (caretY + state.rowHeight > editorScroll.scrollTop + 
editorScroll.clientHeight - 16) {
+2493|      editorScroll.scrollTop = Math.max(0, caretY - 
editorScroll.clientHeight + state.rowHeight * 4);
+2494|    }
+2495|    if (caretX < editorScroll.scrollLeft + 24) {
+2496|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 40);
+2497|    } else if (caretX > editorScroll.scrollLeft + editorScroll.clientWidth
- 24) {
+2498|      editorScroll.scrollLeft = Math.max(0, caretX - 
editorScroll.clientWidth + 80);
+2499|    }
+2500|  }
+2501|
+2502|  function handleEditorMouseDown(event) {
+2503|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+2504|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+2505|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+2506|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+2507|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+2508|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+2509|
+2510|    if (event.altKey || event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) {
+2511|      state.selectionRanges = [...state.selectionRanges, { anchor: offset,
head: offset }];
+2512|      finishGroup();
+2513|      queueRender();
+2514|      return;
+2515|    }
+2516|
+2517|    if (event.detail >= 3) {
+2518|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex], 
head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + line.length }];
+2519|    } else if (event.detail === 2 && segmenterWord) {
+2520|      const relative = offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex];
+2521|      const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
+2522|      let selected = { start: 0, end: line.length };
+2523|      for (const segment of segments) {
+2524|        const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
+2525|        if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index <= relative && relative <=
end) {
+2526|          selected = { start: segment.index, end };
+2527|          break;
+2528|        }
+2529|      }
+2530|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + 
selected.start, head: state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + selected.end }];
+2531|    } else {
+2532|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: offset, head: offset }];
+2533|      dragSelection = { start: offset, active: true };
+2534|    }
+2535|    finishGroup();
+2536|    queueRender();
+2537|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+2538|  }
+2539|
+2540|  function handleEditorMouseMove(event) {
+2541|    if (!dragSelection || !dragSelection.active) {
+2542|      return;
+2543|    }
+2544|    const rect = editorScroll.getBoundingClientRect();
+2545|    const x = editorScroll.scrollLeft + event.clientX - rect.left;
+2546|    const y = editorScroll.scrollTop + event.clientY - rect.top;
+2547|    const lineIndex = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
Math.floor(y / state.rowHeight)));
+2548|    const line = state.lines[lineIndex] || '';
+2549|    const offset = state.lineStarts[lineIndex] + xToOffset(line, 
Math.max(0, x));
+2550|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: dragSelection.start, head: offset 
}];
+2551|    queueRender();
+2552|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+2553|  }
+2554|
+2555|  function finishDragging() {
+2556|    dragSelection = null;
+2557|  }
+2558|
+2559|  function handleEditorShortcut(event) {
+2560|    const isMac = navigator.platform.toLowerCase().includes('mac');
+2561|    const mod = isMac ? event.metaKey : event.ctrlKey;
+2562|    const key = event.key.toLowerCase();
+2563|
+2564|    if (mod && key === 'f') {
+2565|      event.preventDefault();
+2566|      openFindBar();
+2567|      return true;
+2568|    }
+2569|    if (mod && key === 's') {
+2570|      event.preventDefault();
+2571|      saveReport();
+2572|      return true;
+2573|    }
+2574|    if (mod && key === 'z' && !event.shiftKey) {
+2575|      event.preventDefault();
+2576|      handleUndo();
+2577|      return true;
+2578|    }
+2579|    if (mod && (key === 'y' || (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey))) {
+2580|      event.preventDefault();
+2581|      handleRedo();
+2582|      return true;
+2583|    }
+2584|    if (mod && key === 'a') {
+2585|      event.preventDefault();
+2586|      selectAll();
+2587|      return true;
+2588|    }
+2589|    return false;
+2590|  }
+2591|
+2592|  function handleEditorKeyDown(event) {
+2593|    if (handleEditorShortcut(event)) {
+2594|      return;
+2595|    }
+2596|
+2597|    const mod = event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey;
+2598|    const shift = event.shiftKey;
+2599|    const key = event.key;
+2600|
+2601|    if (key === 'Escape') {
+2602|      event.preventDefault();
+2603|      findInput.focus();
+2604|      return;
+2605|    }
+2606|    if (key === 'Tab') {
+2607|      event.preventDefault();
+2608|      insertTab(shift);
+2609|      return;
+2610|    }
+2611|    if (key === 'Enter') {
+2612|      event.preventDefault();
+2613|      typeText('\n');
+2614|      return;
+2615|    }
+2616|    if (key === 'Backspace') {
+2617|      event.preventDefault();
+2618|      deleteByDirection(-1);
+2619|      return;
+2620|    }
+2621|    if (key === 'Delete') {
+2622|      event.preventDefault();
+2623|      deleteByDirection(1);
+2624|      return;
+2625|    }
+2626|    if (key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+2627|      event.preventDefault();
+2628|      moveRanges(-1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+2629|      return;
+2630|    }
+2631|    if (key === 'ArrowRight') {
+2632|      event.preventDefault();
+2633|      moveRanges(1, { shift, byWord: mod });
+2634|      return;
+2635|    }
+2636|    if (key === 'ArrowUp') {
+2637|      event.preventDefault();
+2638|      moveVertical(-1, shift);
+2639|      return;
+2640|    }
+2641|    if (key === 'ArrowDown') {
+2642|      event.preventDefault();
+2643|      moveVertical(1, shift);
+2644|      return;
+2645|    }
+2646|    if (key === 'Home') {
+2647|      event.preventDefault();
+2648|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+2649|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+2650|        const target = mod ? 0 : info.lineStart;
+2651|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+2652|      });
+2653|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+2654|      finishGroup();
+2655|      queueRender();
+2656|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+2657|      return;
+2658|    }
+2659|    if (key === 'End') {
+2660|      event.preventDefault();
+2661|      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
+2662|        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.head);
+2663|        const target = mod ? state.content.length : info.lineStart + 
info.line.length;
+2664|        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: target } : { anchor: 
target, head: target };
+2665|      });
+2666|      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
+2667|      finishGroup();
+2668|      queueRender();
+2669|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+2670|      return;
+2671|    }
+2672|    if (mod && key === 'g') {
+2673|      event.preventDefault();
+2674|      navigateMatches(shift ? -1 : 1);
+2675|      return;
+2676|    }
+2677|    if (key.length === 1 && !mod) {
+2678|      event.preventDefault();
+2679|      typeText(key);
+2680|    }
+2681|  }
+2682|
+2683|  function handlePaste(event) {
+2684|    event.preventDefault();
+2685|    typeText(event.clipboardData?.getData('text/plain') || '');
+2686|  }
+2687|
+2688|  function handleCopy(event) {
+2689|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+2690|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+2691|      return;
+2692|    }
+2693|    event.preventDefault();
+2694|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+2695|  }
+2696|
+2697|  function handleCut(event) {
+2698|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+2699|    if (range.start === range.end) {
+2700|      return;
+2701|    }
+2702|    event.preventDefault();
+2703|    event.clipboardData?.setData('text/plain', 
state.content.slice(range.start, range.end));
+2704|    applyReplacement([range], '', 'cut');
+2705|  }
+2706|
+2707|  function handleFindInputKeyDown(event) {
+2708|    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+2709|      event.preventDefault();
+2710|      editorScroll.focus();
+2711|      return;
+2712|    }
+2713|    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
+2714|      event.preventDefault();
+2715|      navigateMatches(event.shiftKey ? -1 : 1);
+2716|    }
+2717|  }
+2718|
+2719|  function handleScroll() {
+2720|    queueRender();
+2721|  }
+2722|
+2723|  function handlePreviewScroll() {
+2724|    previewScrollState = { top: previewScroll.scrollTop, left: 
previewScroll.scrollLeft };
+2725|    queueRender();
+2726|  }
+2727|
+2728|  function handleWindowResize() {
+2729|    metricsCache.clear();
+2730|    queueRender();
+2731|  }

+2733|  function bindUi() {
+2734|    editorScroll.addEventListener('keydown', handleEditorKeyDown);
+2735|    editorScroll.addEventListener('paste', handlePaste);
+2736|    editorScroll.addEventListener('copy', handleCopy);
+2737|    editorScroll.addEventListener('cut', handleCut);
+2738|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousedown', handleEditorMouseDown);
+2739|    editorScroll.addEventListener('mousemove', handleEditorMouseMove);
+2740|    window.addEventListener('mouseup', finishDragging);
+2741|    editorScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
+2742|    previewScroll.addEventListener('scroll', handlePreviewScroll);
+2743|    findInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
+2744|      state.findQuery = findInput.value;
+2745|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+2746|      findMatches();
+2747|    });
+2748|    findInput.addEventListener('keydown', handleFindInputKeyDown);
+2749|    replaceInput.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => {
+2750|      if (event.key === 'Escape') {
+2751|        editorScroll.focus();
+2752|      }
+2753|    });
+2754|    findToggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
+2755|      findBar.hidden = !findBar.hidden;
+2756|      if (!findBar.hidden) {
+2757|        findInput.focus();
+2758|        findInput.select();
+2759|      } else {
+2760|        editorScroll.focus();
+2761|      }
+2762|    });
+2763|    findPrevButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(-1));
+2764|    findNextButton.addEventListener('click', () => navigateMatches(1));
+2765|    replaceCurrentButton.addEventListener('click', replaceCurrentMatch);
+2766|    replaceAllButton.addEventListener('click', replaceAllMatches);
+2767|    saveButton.addEventListener('click', saveReport);
+2768|    reloadButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+2769|    discardButton.addEventListener('click', reloadLatest);
+2770|    undoButton.addEventListener('click', handleUndo);
+2771|    redoButton.addEventListener('click', handleRedo);
+2772|    closePreviewButton.addEventListener('click', closePreview);
+2773|    previewModal.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+2774|      if (event.target === previewModal || 
event.target.classList.contains('modal-backdrop')) {
+2775|        closePreview();
+2776|      }
+2777|    });
+2778|    window.addEventListener('resize', handleWindowResize);
+2779|  }
+2780|
+2781|  function closePreview() {
+2782|    state.preview = null;
+2783|    previewModal.hidden = true;
+2784|    editorScroll.focus();
+2785|  }
+2786|
+2787|  function reloadLatest() {
+2788|    if (state.document) {
+2789|      loadReport(state.document.id);
+2790|    }
+2791|  }
+2792|
+2793|  function handleUndo() {
+2794|    if (!state.undoStack.length) {
+2795|      setMessage('Nothing to undo.', 'warning');
+2796|      return;
+2797|    }
+2798|    const current = snapshot();
+2799|    const previous = state.undoStack.pop();
+2800|    state.redoStack.push(current);
+2801|    restoreSnapshot(previous);
+2802|    setDirtyStatus();
+2803|    setMessage('Undid the last change.', 'good');
+2804|  }
+2805|
+2806|  function handleRedo() {
+2807|    if (!state.redoStack.length) {
+2808|      setMessage('Nothing to redo.', 'warning');
+2809|      return;
+2810|    }
+2811|    const current = snapshot();
+2812|    const next = state.redoStack.pop();
+2813|    state.undoStack.push(current);
+2814|    restoreSnapshot(next);
+2815|    setDirtyStatus();
+2816|    setMessage('Redid the last change.', 'good');
+2817|  }
+2818|
+2819|  function insertTab(shiftKey) {
+2820|    const range = getPrimaryRange();
+2821|    if (range.start !== range.end && state.content.slice(range.start, 
range.end).includes('\n')) {
+2822|      indentSelection(shiftKey);
+2823|      return;
+2824|    }
+2825|    if (shiftKey) {
+2826|      indentSelection(true);
+2827|      return;
+2828|    }
+2829|    typeText('\t');
+2830|  }
+2831|
+2832|  function openFindBar() {
+2833|    findBar.hidden = false;
+2834|    findInput.focus();
+2835|    findInput.select();
+2836|  }
+2837|
+2838|  function loadReport(reportId) {
+2839|    if (!reportId) {
+2840|      return Promise.resolve();
+2841|    }
+2842|    setStatus('Loading report…');
+2843|    setMessage('');
+2844|    return fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}`)
+2845|      .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, data
})))

+2914|  function saveReport() {
+2915|    if (!state.document) {
+2916|      return;
+2917|    }
+2918|    setMessage('Saving report…');
+2919|    fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/save`, {
+2920|      method: 'POST',
+2921|      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
+2922|      body: JSON.stringify({ documentId: state.document.id, baseRevision: 
state.baseRevision, content: state.content }),
+2923|    })
+2924|      .then(async (response) => {
+2925|        const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
+2926|        if (response.status === 409) {
+2927|          state.conflict = data.document || { currentRevision: 'unknown' 
};
+2928|          setDirtyStatus();
+2929|          setMessage('Save conflict: another tab saved a newer version. 
Your draft is preserved.', 'warning');
+2930|          return null;
+2931|        }
+2932|        if (!response.ok) {
+2933|          throw new Error(data.error || 'Save failed.');
+2934|        }
+2935|        return data;
+2936|      })
+2937|      .then((data) => {
+2938|        if (!data) {
+2939|          return;
+2940|        }
+2941|        state.conflict = null;
+2942|        state.savedContent = data.document.content;
+2943|        state.baseRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
+2944|        state.document.currentRevision = data.document.currentRevision;
+2945|        state.document.updatedAt = data.document.updatedAt;
+2946|        renderReports();
+2947|        renderHistory();
+2948|        setDirtyStatus();
+2949|        setMessage(data.changed ? `Saved as revision 
${state.baseRevision}.` : 'No changes to save.', 'good');
+2950|      })
+2951|      .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
+2952|  }
+2953|
+2954|  function replaceCurrentMatch() {
+2955|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+2956|      setMessage('No match to replace.', 'warning');
+2957|      return;
+2958|    }
+2959|    const match = state.matches[state.activeMatchIndex < 0 ? 0 : 
state.activeMatchIndex];
+2960|    const before = snapshot();
+2961|    pushUndo(before, 'replace-current');
+2962|    updateContentModel(state.content.slice(0, match.start) + 
replaceInput.value + state.content.slice(match.end));
+2963|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start + 
replaceInput.value.length, head: match.start + replaceInput.value.length }];
+2964|    findMatches();
+2965|    queueRender();
+2966|    setDirtyStatus();
+2967|    setMessage('Replaced the current match.', 'good');
+2968|  }
+2969|
+2970|  function replaceAllMatches() {
+2971|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+2972|      setMessage('No matches to replace.', 'warning');
+2973|      return;
+2974|    }
+2975|    const before = snapshot();
+2976|    pushUndo(before, 'replace-all');
+2977|    const replacement = replaceInput.value;
+2978|    let content = state.content;
+2979|    for (let index = state.matches.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
+2980|      const match = state.matches[index];
+2981|      content = content.slice(0, match.start) + replacement + 
content.slice(match.end);
+2982|    }
+2983|    updateContentModel(content);
+2984|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
+2985|    findMatches();
+2986|    queueRender();
+2987|    setDirtyStatus();
+2988|    setMessage(`Replaced all ${before.matches.length} matches.`, 'good');
+2989|  }

+3007|  function handleFindSelection() {
+3008|    if (!state.findQuery) {
+3009|      state.matches = [];
+3010|      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+3011|      renderFindBar();
+3012|      queueRender();
+3013|      return;
+3014|    }
+3015|    const matches = [];
+3016|    let index = state.content.indexOf(state.findQuery);
+3017|    while (index !== -1) {
+3018|      matches.push({ start: index, end: index + state.findQuery.length });
+3019|      index = state.content.indexOf(state.findQuery, index + Math.max(1, 
state.findQuery.length));
+3020|    }
+3021|    state.matches = matches;
+3022|    if (!matches.length) {
+3023|      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
+3024|    } else if (state.activeMatchIndex < 0 || state.activeMatchIndex >= 
matches.length) {
+3025|      state.activeMatchIndex = 0;
+3026|    }
+3027|    renderFindBar();
+3028|    if (matches.length) {
+3029|      const match = matches[state.activeMatchIndex];
+3030|      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }];
+3031|      scrollSelectionIntoView();
+3032|    }
+3033|    queueRender();
+3034|  }
+3035|
+3036|  function navigateMatches(direction) {
+3037|    if (!state.matches.length) {
+3038|      setMessage('No matches found.', 'warning');
+3039|      return;
+3040|    }
+3041|    const total = state.matches.length;
+3042|    state.activeMatchIndex = (state.activeMatchIndex + direction + total) 
% total;
+3043|    const match = state.matches[state.activeMatchIndex];
+3044|    state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: match.start, head: match.end }];
+3045|    renderFindBar();
+3046|    scrollSelectionIntoView();
+3047|    queueRender();
+3048|    setMessage(`Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of ${total}.`, 
'good');
+3049|  }
+3050|
+3051|  function findMatches() {
+3052|    handleFindSelection();
+3053|  }

    25  function documentTextFromSeed(seed) {
    26    const doc = seed.document;
    27    const generatedLines = Array.from({ length: doc.generatedLineCount }, 
(_, index) =>
    28      doc.generatedLineTemplate.replace('{n}', String(index + 1))
    29    );
    30    return [...doc.sections, ...generatedLines, 
...doc.tailSections].join('\n');
    31  }

    33  function initializeSchema() {
    34    db.exec(`
    35      PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
    36      PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
    37      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS documents (
    38        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    39        title TEXT NOT NULL,
    40        author TEXT NOT NULL,
    41        summary TEXT NOT NULL,
    42        content TEXT NOT NULL,
    43        current_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,
    44        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    45        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
    46      );
    47      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revisions (
    48        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    49        document_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    50        revision_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
    51        content TEXT NOT NULL,
    52        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    53        UNIQUE(document_id, revision_number),
    54        FOREIGN KEY(document_id) REFERENCES documents(id) ON DELETE 
CASCADE
    55      );
    56    `);

    82    function cloneRanges(ranges) {
    83      return ranges.map((range) => ({ anchor: range.anchor, head:

    86    function normalizedRange(range) {
    87      return range.anchor <= range.head
    88        ? { start: range.anchor, end: range.head, anchor: range.anchor, 
head: range.head }
    89        : { start: range.head, end: range.anchor, anchor: range.anchor, 
head: range.head };
    90    }

    92    function sortRanges(ranges) {
    93      return ranges.map(normalizedRange).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start 
|| a.end - b.end);
    94    }

    96    function dedupeCollapsed(ranges) {
    97      const seen = new Set();
    98      const result = [];
    99      for (const range of ranges) {
   100        if (range.start === range.end) {
   101          const key = String(range.start);
   102          if (seen.has(key)) {
   103            continue;
   104          }
   105          seen.add(key);
   106        }
   107        result.push(range);
   108      }
   109      return result;
   110    }

   171    function pushUndo(snapshotData, kind) {
   172      const group = state.typingGroup;
   173      const now = Date.now();

   188    function ensureLineMetrics(line) {
   189      const cached = metricsCache.get(line);
   190      if (cached) {
   191        return cached;
   192      }
   193      measureContext.font = getComputedStyle(editorScroll).font;
   194      const offsets = [0];
   195      const widths = [0];
   196      let offset = 0;
   197      let width = 0;
   198      const segments = segmenterGrapheme ? 
Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(line), (part) => part.segment) :

   199      for (const segment of segments) {
   200        offset += segment.length;
   201        const display = segment === '\t' ? '    ' : segment;
   202        width += measureContext.measureText(display).width;
   203        offsets.push(offset);

   205      }
   206      const metrics = { offsets, widths, width };
   207      metricsCache.set(line, metrics);
   208      return metrics;
   209    }
   210  
   211    function offsetToX(line, offset) {
   212      const metrics = ensureLineMetrics(line);
   213      let low = 0;
   214      let high = metrics.offsets.length - 1;
   215      while (low <= high) {
   216        const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
   217        if (metrics.offsets[mid] === offset) {
   218          return metrics.widths[mid];
   219        }
   220        if (metrics.offsets[mid] < offset) {
   221          low = mid + 1;
   222        } else {
   223          high = mid - 1;
   224        }
   225      }
   226      return metrics.widths[Math.max(0, high)];
   227    }
   228  
   229    function xToOffset(line, x) {
   230      const metrics = ensureLineMetrics(line);
   231      let low = 0;
   232      let high = metrics.widths.length - 1;
   233      while (low <= high) {
   234        const mid = (low + high) >> 1;
   235        if (metrics.widths[mid] === x) {
   236          return metrics.offsets[mid];
   237        }
   238        if (metrics.widths[mid] < x) {
   239          low = mid + 1;
   240        } else {
   241          high = mid - 1;
   242        }
   243      }
   244      return metrics.offsets[Math.max(0, high)];
   245    }

   255      }

   257        lineIndex,
   258        lineStart: state.lineStarts[lineIndex],
   259        line: state.lines[lineIndex] || '',
   260        columnOffset: offset - state.lineStarts[lineIndex],

   264    function nextGrapheme(line, offset, direction) {
   265      if (direction > 0) {
   266        if (offset >= line.length) {
   267          return line.length;
   268        }
   269        const rest = line.slice(offset);
   270        const segments = segmenterGrapheme ? 
Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(rest), (part) => part.segment) : 
Array.from(rest);
   271        return offset + (segments[0]?.length || 1);
   272      }
   273      if (offset <= 0) {
   274        return 0;
   275      }
   276      const part = line.slice(0, offset);
   277      const segments = segmenterGrapheme ? 
Array.from(segmenterGrapheme.segment(part), (seg) => seg.segment) : 
Array.from(part);
   278      return offset - (segments[segments.length - 1]?.length || 1);
   279    }
   280  
   281    function nextWordBoundary(line, offset, direction) {
   282      if (!segmenterWord) {
   283        return nextGrapheme(line, offset, direction);
   284      }
   285      const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));
   286      if (direction > 0) {
   287        for (const segment of segments) {
   288          const end = segment.index + segment.segment.length;
   289          if (segment.index > offset) {
   290            return segment.index;
   291          }
   292          if (segment.index <= offset && offset < end) {
   293            return end;
   294          }
   295        }
   296        return line.length;
   297      }
   298      for (let index = segments.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   299        const segment = segments[index];
   300        if (segment.isWordLike && segment.index < offset) {
   301          return segment.index;
   302        }
   303      }
   304      return 0;
   305    }

   307    function selectAll() {
   308      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: state.content.length }];
   309      finishGroup();
   310      queueRender();
   311      scrollSelectionIntoView();
   312    }
   313  
   314    function applyReplacement(ranges, replacement, kind) {
   315      const sorted = sortRanges(ranges);
   316      if (!sorted.length) {
   317        return;
   318      }
   319      const before = snapshot();
   320      pushUndo(before, kind);
   321      let content = state.content;
   322      const nextSelections = [];
   323      for (let index = sorted.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
   324        const range = sorted[index];
   325        content = content.slice(0, range.start) + replacement + 
content.slice(range.end);
   326        nextSelections.unshift({ anchor: range.start + replacement.length,
head: range.start + replacement.length });
   327      }
   328      updateContentModel(content);
   329      state.selectionRanges = nextSelections;
   330      state.matches = [];
   331      state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
   332      renderFindBar();
   333      queueRender();
   334    }

   336    function typeText(text) {
   337      applyReplacement(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges), text, 'insert');
   338    }

   340    function deleteByDirection(direction) {
   341      const ranges = sortRanges(state.selectionRanges).map((range) => {
   342        if (range.start !== range.end) {
   343          return range;
   344        }
   345        const info = lineInfoForOffset(range.start);
   346        const relative = range.start - info.lineStart;
   347        const line = info.line;
   348        const nextRelative = direction < 0 ? nextGrapheme(line, relative, 
-1) : nextGrapheme(line, relative, 1);
   349        return direction < 0
   350          ? { start: info.lineStart + nextRelative, end: range.start, 
anchor: range.start, head: range.start }
   351          : { start: range.start, end: info.lineStart + nextRelative, 
anchor: range.start, head: range.start };
   352      });
   353      applyReplacement(ranges, '', direction < 0 ? 'backspace' : 
'delete');
   354    }
   355  
   356    function moveRanges(direction, { shift = false, byWord = false } = {})
{
   357      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
   358        const normalized = normalizedRange(range);
   359        const collapseTarget = shift ? range.head : (direction > 0 ? 
normalized.end : normalized.start);
   360        const info = lineInfoForOffset(collapseTarget);
   361        const relative = collapseTarget - info.lineStart;
   362        const nextRelative = byWord ? nextWordBoundary(info.line, 
relative, direction) : nextGrapheme(info.line, relative, direction);
   363        const nextOffset = info.lineStart + nextRelative;
   364        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: nextOffset } : { 
anchor: nextOffset, head: nextOffset };
   365      });
   366      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
   367      finishGroup();
   368      queueRender();
   369      scrollSelectionIntoView();
   370    }
   371  
   372    function moveVertical(direction, shift = false) {
   373      state.selectionRanges = state.selectionRanges.map((range) => {
   374        const current = shift ? range.head : normalizedRange(range).end;
   375        const info = lineInfoForOffset(current);
   376        const nextLine = Math.max(0, Math.min(state.lines.length - 1, 
info.lineIndex + direction));
   377        const line = state.lines[nextLine] || '';
   378        const nextColumn = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
   379        const nextMetrics = ensureLineMetrics(line);
   380        const nextRelative = nextMetrics.offsets[Math.min(nextColumn, 
nextMetrics.offsets.length - 1)];
   381        const nextOffset = state.lineStarts[nextLine] + nextRelative;
   382        return shift ? { anchor: range.anchor, head: nextOffset } : { 
anchor: nextOffset, head: nextOffset };
   383      });
   384      state.selectionRanges = 
dedupeCollapsed(sortRanges(state.selectionRanges));
   385      finishGroup();
   386      queueRender();
   387      scrollSelectionIntoView();
   388    }
   389  
   390    function currentSelectionText() {
   391      const range = getPrimaryRange();
   392      return state.content.slice(range.start, range.end);
   393    }
   394  
   395    function indentSelection(outdent = false) {
   396      const range = getPrimaryRange();
   397      const startLine = lineInfoForOffset(range.start).lineIndex;
   398      const endLine = lineInfoForOffset(range.end).lineIndex;
   399      const lines = state.lines.slice(startLine, endLine + 1);
   400      if (!lines.length) {
   401        return;
   402      }
   403      const before = snapshot();
   404      pushUndo(before, outdent ? 'outdent' : 'indent');
   405      const adjusted = lines.map((line) => {
   406        if (outdent) {
   407          if (line.startsWith('\t')) {
   408            return line.slice(1);
   409          }
   410          if (line.startsWith('    ')) {
   411            return line.slice(4);
   412          }
   413          if (line.startsWith(' ')) {
   414            return line.replace(/^ +/, (match) => match.slice(Math.min(4, 
match.length)));
   415          }
   416          return line;
   417        }
   418        return `\t${line}`;
   419      });
   420      const startOffset = state.lineStarts[startLine];
   421      const endOffset = endLine < state.lines.length - 1 ? 
state.lineStarts[endLine + 1] : state.content.length;
   422      updateContentModel(state.content.slice(0, startOffset) + 
adjusted.join('\n') + state.content.slice(endOffset));
   423      state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: startOffset, head: startOffset + 
adjusted.join('\n').length }];
   424      queueRender();
   425      setDirtyStatus();
   426    }
   427  
   428    function openPreview(revisionNumber) {
   429      
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
   430        .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, 
data })))
   431        .then(({ response, data }) => {
   432          if (!response.ok) {
   433            throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to preview revision.');
   434          }
   435          state.preview = data;
   436          previewMeta.textContent = `Revision ${data.revisionNumber} • 
${formatDate(data.createdAt)}`;
   437          previewModal.hidden = false;
   438          previewScroll.scrollTop = 0;
   439          previewScroll.scrollLeft = 0;
   440          renderPreview();
   441        })
   442        .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
   443    }
   444  
   445    function restoreRevision(revisionNumber) {
   446      
fetch(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(state.document.id)}/revisions/${encodeU
RIComponent(revisionNumber)}`)
   447        .then((response) => response.json().then((data) => ({ response, 
data })))
   448        .then(({ response, data }) => {
   449          if (!response.ok) {
   450            throw new Error(data.error || 'Unable to restore revision.');
   451          }
   452          const before = snapshot();
   453          pushUndo(before, 'restore');
   454          updateContentModel(data.content);
   455          state.selectionRanges = [{ anchor: 0, head: 0 }];
   456          state.matches = [];
   457          state.activeMatchIndex = -1;
   458          renderFindBar();
   459          queueRender();
   460          setDirtyStatus();
   461          setMessage(`Restored revision ${data.revisionNumber} into the 
editor. Save to keep it.`, 'warning');
   462        })
   463        .catch((error) => setMessage(error.message, 'error'));
   464    }

   477        return;
   478      }

${state.document.summary}`;

   483    function renderHistory() {
   484      if (!state.revisions.length) {
   485        historyList.innerHTML = '<div class="history-entry">No revisions 
available.</div>';
   486        return;
   487      }
   488      historyList.innerHTML = state.revisions.map((revision) => `
   489        <article class="history-entry">
   490          <div>
   491            <strong>Revision ${revision.revisionNumber}</strong>
   492            <div><small>${formatDate(revision.createdAt)}</small></div>
   493          </div>
   494          <div class="history-entry-actions">
   495            <button type="button" 
data-preview-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Preview</button>
   496            <button type="button" 
data-restore-revision="${revision.revisionNumber}">Restore</button>
   497          </div>
   498        </article>
   499      `).join('');
   500      
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-preview-revision]').forEach((button) => {
   501        button.onclick = () => 
openPreview(button.dataset.previewRevision);
   502      });
   503      
historyList.querySelectorAll('[data-restore-revision]').forEach((button) => {
   504        button.onclick = () => 
restoreRevision(button.dataset.restoreRevision);
   505      });
   506    }

   508    function renderFindBar() {
   509      findCount.textContent = `${state.matches.length} 
match${state.matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'es'}`;
   510      if (!state.findQuery) {
   511        findStatus.textContent = 'Search the open report.';
   512      } else if (!state.matches.length) {
   513        findStatus.textContent = 'No matches.';
   514      } else {
   515        findStatus.textContent = `Match ${state.activeMatchIndex + 1} of 
${state.matches.length}`;
   516      }
   517    }
   518  
   519    function setDirtyStatus() {
   520      if (state.conflict) {
   521        setStatus(`Save conflict: server is at revision 
${state.conflict.currentRevision}.`, 'warning');
   522        return;
   523      }
   524      if (state.content === state.savedContent) {
   525        setStatus(`Saved at revision ${state.baseRevision}.`, 'good');
   526      } else {
   527        setStatus(`Unsaved changes • base revision ${state.baseRevision}`,
'warning');
   528      }
   529    }
   530  
   531    function renderStatus() {
   532      setDirtyStatus();
   533      const info = lineInfoForOffset(getPrimaryRange().head);
   534      const column = Array.from(segmenterGrapheme ? 
segmenterGrapheme.segment(info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)) : 
info.line.slice(0, info.columnOffset)).length;
   535      cursorState.textContent = state.selectionRanges.length > 1
   536        ? `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1} • 
${state.selectionRanges.length} carets`
   537        : `Line ${info.lineIndex + 1}, Column ${column + 1}`;
   538    }
   539  
   540    function renderEditorSurface(scrollContainer, gutterContainer, 
content, mode) {
   541      const rowHeight = state.rowHeight;
   542      const scrollTop = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.top : 
scrollContainer.scrollTop;
   543      const scrollLeft = mode === 'preview' ? previewScrollState.left : 
scrollContainer.scrollLeft;
   544      const visibleHeight = scrollContainer.clientHeight || 0;
   545      const startLine = Math.max(0, Math.floor(scrollTop / rowHeight) - 
2);
   546      const endLine = Math.min(content.lines.length - 1, 
Math.ceil((scrollTop + visibleHeight) / rowHeight) + 2);
   547      const totalHeight = content.lines.length * rowHeight;
   548      const ranges = content.selectionRanges || [];
   549      const matches = content.matches || [];
   550      const activeMatchIndex = content.activeMatchIndex ?? -1;
   551  
   552      const rows = [];
   553      const gutterRows = [];
   554      let maxWidth = 0;
   555      for (let lineIndex = startLine; lineIndex <= endLine; lineIndex += 
1) {
   556        const line = content.lines[lineIndex] || '';
   557        const lineStart = content.lineStarts[lineIndex] || 0;
   558        const lineWidth = ensureLineMetrics(line).width;
   559        maxWidth = Math.max(maxWidth, lineWidth);
   560        const top = lineIndex * rowHeight;
   561        const selectionRects = [];
   562        const matchRects = [];
   563        const carets = [];
   564  
   565        for (const range of ranges) {
   566          const start = Math.max(range.start, lineStart);
   567          const end = Math.min(range.end, lineStart + line.length);
   568          if (start < end) {
   569            const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
   570            selectionRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line,
end - lineStart) - left) });
   571          }
   572          if (range.start === range.end && range.start >= lineStart && 
range.start <= lineStart + line.length) {
   573            carets.push({ left: offsetToX(line, range.start - lineStart) 
});
   574          }
   575        }
   576  
   577        for (let index = 0; index < matches.length; index += 1) {
   578          const match = matches[index];
   579          const start = Math.max(match.start, lineStart);
   580          const end = Math.min(match.end, lineStart + line.length);
   581          if (start < end) {
   582            const left = offsetToX(line, start - lineStart);
   583            matchRects.push({ left, width: Math.max(1, offsetToX(line, end
- lineStart) - left), current: index === activeMatchIndex });
   584          }
   585        }
   586  
   587        rows.push(`
   588          <div class="line-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;">
   589            ${matchRects.map((rect) => `<div 
class="match-rect${rect.current ? ' current-match' : ''}" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
   590            ${selectionRects.map((rect) => `<div class="selection-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px; width:${rect.width}px;"></div>`).join('')}
   591            ${carets.map((rect) => `<div class="cursor-rect" 
style="left:${rect.left}px;"></div>`).join('')}
   592            <span class="line-text">${escapeHtml(line)}</span>
   593          </div>
   594        `);
   595        gutterRows.push(`<div class="gutter-row" style="top:${top}px; 
height:${rowHeight}px;"><span class="line-number">${lineIndex + 
1}</span></div>`);
   596      }
   597  
   598      scrollContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="editor-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px; min-width:${maxWidth + 
200}px;">${rows.join('')}</div>`;
   599      gutterContainer.innerHTML = `<div class="gutter-content" 
style="height:${totalHeight}px;">${gutterRows.join('')}</div>`;
   600      scrollContainer.scrollTop = scrollTop;
   601      scrollContainer.scrollLeft = scrollLeft;
   602    }
   603  
   604    function renderEditor() {
   605      if (!state.document) {
   606        return;
   607      }
   608      renderEditorSurface(editorScroll, lineGutter, {
   609        lines: state.lines,
   610        lineStarts: state.lineStarts,
   611        selectionRanges: state.selectionRanges,
   612        matches: state.matches,
   613        activeMatchIndex: state.activeMatchIndex,
   614      }, 'editor');
   615      renderStatus();
   616    }

   619      if (!state.preview) {
   620        return;

      function nextWordBoundary(line, offset, direction) {
        if (!segmenterWord) {
          return nextGrapheme(line, offset, direction);
        }
        const segments = Array.from(segmenterWord.segment(line));

          }

        return 0;
